````text
/**
 * WHAT: Starts a Codex run from the active thread panel and refreshes the canvas.
 * WHY: The created run widget is durable card state authored by the backend.
 */
import { state } from '../../state.js';
import { refreshRuntimeState } from '../../refresh/controller/refresh-runtime-state.js';
import { telemetry } from '../../telemetry/effect/telemetry.js';
import { requestThreadCodexProcess } from '../effect/request-thread-codex-process.js';
import { threadCodexCardId } from '../helper/thread-codex-card-id.js';

export async function processThreadCodexController(input: { threadId?: string; cardId?: string; codexModel?: string; codexEffort?: string } = {}): Promise<boolean> {
  const ledgerId = String(state.activeTab ?? '').trim();
  const threadId = String(input.threadId ?? state.threadId ?? '').trim();
  const cardId = String(input.cardId ?? '').trim() || threadCodexCardId(state.activeLedger, threadId);
  if (!ledgerId || !threadId || !cardId) return false;
  telemetry('codex-thread-process-start', { ledgerId, threadId, cardId, codexModel: input.codexModel ?? '', codexEffort: input.codexEffort ?? '' });
  const result = await requestThreadCodexProcess({ ledgerId, threadId, cardId, codexModel: input.codexModel, codexEffort: input.codexEffort });
  if (!result.ok) {
    telemetry('codex-thread-process-failed', { ledgerId, threadId, cardId, codexModel: input.codexModel ?? '', codexEffort: input.codexEffort ?? '', error: result.error ?? '' });
    return false;
  }
  await refreshRuntimeState();
  telemetry('codex-thread-process-created-widget', { ledgerId, threadId, cardId, codexModel: input.codexModel ?? '', codexEffort: input.codexEffort ?? '', run: result.run?.id ?? '' });
  return true;
}
````

````text
    63	function normalizedContentFile(value: unknown): string {
    64	  const file = String(value ?? '').trim().replace(/\\/g, '/');
    65	  if (file.startsWith('/.decision-os/')) return file.slice(1);
    66	  return file.replace(/^\.\/+/, '');
    67	}
    68	
    69	export function changedCardIdForContentFile(contentFile: string): string {
    70	  const target = normalizedContentFile(contentFile);
    71	  if (!target) return '';
    72	  const cards = Array.isArray(state.activeLedger?.cards) ? state.activeLedger.cards as Array<Record<string, unknown>> : [];
    73	  for (const card of cards) {
    74	    const comment = card.comment && typeof card.comment === 'object' ? card.comment as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    75	    if (normalizedContentFile(comment.contentFile) === target) return String(card.id ?? '');
    76	  }
    77	  return '';
    78	}

    80	async function resizeChangedCardToContent(contentFile: string): Promise<void> {
    81	  const cardId = changedCardIdForContentFile(contentFile);
    82	  if (!cardId) {
    83	    telemetry('ledger-content-refresh-resize-skipped', { reason: 'card-not-found', contentFile });
    84	    return;
    85	  }
    86	  const geometry = resizeSelectedCardsToContent({ cardIds: [cardId], zoneIds: [] });
    87	  if (Object.keys(geometry.cards).length === 0 && Object.keys(geometry.zones).length === 0) {
    88	    telemetry('ledger-content-refresh-resize-skipped', { reason: 'empty-geometry', contentFile, cardId });
    89	    return;
    90	  }
    91	
    92	  persistState();
    93	  const committed = state.activeLedger
    94	    ? await commitActiveLedgerMutation({ action: 'patch-geometry', geometry }, { render: true })
    95	    : false;
    96	  telemetry('ledger-content-refresh-resize', { contentFile, cardId, committed });
    97	}
    98	
    99	async function reloadLedgerContent(reason: string, options: LedgerRefreshOptions = {}): Promise<void> {
   100	  if (refreshInFlight) {
   101	    state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh = true;
   102	    return;
   103	  }
   104	  refreshInFlight = true;
   105	  try {
   106	    await loadActiveLedgerState();
   107	    renderCanvasSurface();
   108	    if (options.contentFile) await resizeChangedCardToContent(options.contentFile);
   109	    telemetry('ledger-content-refresh', { reason, contentFile: options.contentFile ?? '' });
   110	  } finally {
   111	    refreshInFlight = false;
   112	  }
   113	}
   114	
   115	async function reloadThreadContent(reason: string): Promise<void> {
   116	  if (threadRefreshInFlight) {
   117	    state.pendingThreadContentRefresh = true;
   118	    return;
   119	  }
   120	  threadRefreshInFlight = true;
   121	  const selectionAtRefreshStart = cloneSelectionState(state.selection);
   122	  try {
   123	    await loadActiveLedgerState();
   124	    // WHAT: Restore a detached clone only when the awaited load did not observe newer operator state.
   125	    // WHY: A stale thread refresh must never overwrite a selection made while its request was in flight.
   126	    if (!state.pointer && selectionStatesEqual(state.selection, selectionAtRefreshStart)) {
   127	      state.selection = cloneSelectionState(selectionAtRefreshStart);
   128	    }
   129	    renderThreadPanel();
   130	    telemetry('thread-content-refresh', { reason });
   131	  } finally {
   132	    threadRefreshInFlight = false;
   133	  }
   134	}
   135	
   136	export function requestLedgerContentRefresh(reason = 'card-content-change', options: LedgerRefreshOptions = {}): void {
   137	  if (state.voice?.recording) {
   138	    state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh = true;
   139	    telemetry('ledger-content-refresh-deferred', { reason, voiceRecording: true });
   140	    return;
   141	  }
   142	  state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh = false;
   143	  void reloadLedgerContent(reason, options);
   144	}
   145	
   146	export function requestThreadContentRefresh(reason = 'thread-content-change'): void {
   147	  if (state.voice?.recording) {
   148	    state.pendingThreadContentRefresh = true;
   149	    telemetry('thread-content-refresh-deferred', { reason, voiceRecording: true });
   150	    return;
   151	  }
   152	  state.pendingThreadContentRefresh = false;
   153	  void reloadThreadContent(reason);
   154	}
   155	
   156	export function flushPendingLedgerContentRefresh(reason = 'voice-recording-stopped'): void {
   157	  if (state.voice?.recording) return;
   158	  if (state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh) {
   159	    state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh = false;
   160	    void reloadLedgerContent(reason);
   161	  }
   162	  if (state.pendingThreadContentRefresh) {
   163	    state.pendingThreadContentRefresh = false;
   164	    void reloadThreadContent(reason);
   165	  }
   166	}
   167	
   168	export function subscribeLedgerContentEvents(): void {
   169	  if (subscribed || typeof EventSource === 'undefined') return;
   170	  subscribed = true;
   171	  const events = new EventSource('/api/ledger-content-events');
   172	  events.addEventListener('card-content-change', (event) => {
   173	    const payload = contentEventPayload(event);
   174	    if (payload.kind === 'thread-content') {
   175	      requestThreadContentRefresh('thread-content-change');
   176	      return;
   177	    }
   178	    requestLedgerContentRefresh('card-content-change', { contentFile: payload.contentFile });
   179	  });
   180	  events.addEventListener('ledger-content-change', (event) => {
   181	    const payload = contentEventPayload(event);
   182	    maybeResumeCodexRunWidget(payload);
   183	    requestLedgerContentRefresh(payload.reason || 'ledger-content-change');
   184	  });
   185	  events.onerror = () => {
   186	    telemetry('ledger-content-refresh-stream-error', {});
   187	  };
   188	  state.ledgerContentEventSource = events;
   189	  telemetry('subscribe-ledger-content-events', {});
   190	}
````

````text
   445	export function resumeExternallyStartedCardSkillRun(input: { ledgerId: string; cardId: string; runId: string }): boolean {
   446	  const key = pollerKey(input);
   447	  terminalSummaries.delete(key);
   448	  const poller = pollers.get(key);
   449	  if (!poller) return false;
   450	  poller.continueInFlight = false;
   451	  paintExternallyStartedRun(poller);
   452	  pollers.set(key, poller);
   453	  schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   454	  return true;
   455	}
   456	
   457	export function bindCardSkillRunWidget(input: { ledgerId: string; cardId: string; runId: string; element: HTMLElement }): void {
   458	  const key = pollerKey(input);
   459	  const terminalSummary = terminalSummaries.get(key);
   460	  if (terminalSummary) {
   461	    const poller: Poller = { ...input, since: terminalSummary.lineCount, startedAtMs: runStartedAt(input.runId), timer: null, clock: null, lastClockPaintMs: 0, inFlight: false, cancelInFlight: false, continueInFlight: false, continueTraceId: '', detachedChecks: 0, terminal: true };
   462	    pollers.set(key, poller);
   463	    paintWidget(input.element, terminalSummary);
   464	    bindCancelButton(poller);
   465	    bindContinueButton(poller);
   466	    bindNewSessionButton(poller);
   467	    return;
   468	  }
   469	  const existing = pollers.get(key);
   470	  if (existing) {
   471	    existing.element = input.element;
   472	    existing.ledgerId = input.ledgerId;
   473	    existing.cardId = input.cardId;
   474	    existing.runId = input.runId;
   475	    existing.terminal = false;
   476	    bindCancelButton(existing);
   477	    bindContinueButton(existing);
   478	    bindNewSessionButton(existing);
   479	    startFrontendClock(existing);
   480	    if (!existing.timer && !existing.inFlight) schedulePoll(existing, 0);
   481	    return;
   482	  }
   483	  const poller: Poller = { ...input, since: 0, startedAtMs: runStartedAt(input.runId), timer: null, clock: null, lastClockPaintMs: 0, inFlight: false, cancelInFlight: false, continueInFlight: false, continueTraceId: '', detachedChecks: 0, terminal: false };
   484	  pollers.set(key, poller);
   485	  bindCancelButton(poller);
   486	  bindContinueButton(poller);
   487	  bindNewSessionButton(poller);
   488	  startFrontendClock(poller);
   489	  schedulePoll(poller, 0);
   490	}
````

# AGENT
<!-- decision-os:note {"id":"codex-codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa-line-90","timestamp":"2026-07-10T06:30:14.198Z","status":"Codex message","codexRunId":"codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa","codexLine":"90","codexKind":"agent_message","codexEventType":"item.completed","codexItemId":"item_45"} -->

The 1-second timer is not directly replacing canvas state: `poll-card-skill-run.ts` only updates the run widget. The damaging chain is indirect: each poll can persist new Codex events into the thread, the file watcher emits `thread-content-change`, and `reloadThreadContent()` reloads the entire active ledger before rebuilding the thread panel. That explains why model/effort controls can become unclickable mid-interaction and why the earlier audit, which focused on canvas refresh, missed a distinct thread-control remount path. I’m tracing the geometry rollback race in that same chain now.

````text
     1	type AnyRecord = Record<string, any>;
     2	type MergeLocalCanvasStateOptions = {
     3	  skipCardIds?: Iterable<string>;
     4	  skipAnnotationIds?: Iterable<string>;
     5	};
     6	
     7	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is AnyRecord {
     8	  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
     9	}
    10	
    11	function finiteNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
    12	  const number = Number(value);
    13	  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
    14	}
    15	
    16	function byId(records: unknown): Map<string, AnyRecord> {
    17	  const entries = Array.isArray(records) ? records as AnyRecord[] : [];
    18	  return new Map(entries.flatMap((record) => {
    19	    const id = String(record?.id ?? '');
    20	    return id ? [[id, record] as [string, AnyRecord]] : [];
    21	  }));
    22	}
    23	
    24	function stringSet(values: Iterable<string> | undefined): Set<string> {
    25	  return new Set(Array.from(values ?? [], (value) => String(value)));
    26	}
    27	
    28	function copyCardGeometry(local: AnyRecord, incoming: AnyRecord): void {
    29	  const x = finiteNumber(local.x);
    30	  const y = finiteNumber(local.y);
    31	  const width = finiteNumber(local.w ?? local.width);
    32	  const height = finiteNumber(local.h ?? local.height);
    33	  if (x !== null) incoming.x = x;
    34	  if (y !== null) incoming.y = y;
    35	  if (width !== null) {
    36	    if ('width' in incoming && !('w' in incoming)) incoming.width = width;
    37	    else incoming.w = width;
    38	  }
    39	  if (height !== null) {
    40	    if ('height' in incoming && !('h' in incoming)) incoming.height = height;
    41	    else incoming.h = height;
    42	  }
    43	}
    44	
    45	function copyAnnotationGeometry(local: AnyRecord, incoming: AnyRecord): void {
    46	  const x = finiteNumber(local.x);
    47	  const y = finiteNumber(local.y);
    48	  const width = finiteNumber(local.width ?? local.w);
    49	  const height = finiteNumber(local.height ?? local.h);
    50	  if (x !== null) incoming.x = x;
    51	  if (y !== null) incoming.y = y;
    52	  if (width !== null) incoming.width = width;
    53	  if (height !== null) incoming.height = height;
    54	}
    55	
    56	export function mergeLocalCanvasStateIntoLedger(incomingLedger: unknown, localLedger: unknown, options: MergeLocalCanvasStateOptions = {}): unknown {
    57	  if (!isRecord(incomingLedger) || !isRecord(localLedger)) return incomingLedger;
    58	  const localCards = byId(localLedger.cards);
    59	  const localAnnotations = byId(localLedger.annotations);
    60	  const skipCardIds = stringSet(options.skipCardIds);
    61	  const skipAnnotationIds = stringSet(options.skipAnnotationIds);
    62	  if (Array.isArray(incomingLedger.cards)) {
    63	    for (const incomingCard of incomingLedger.cards as AnyRecord[]) {
    64	      const cardId = String(incomingCard.id ?? '');
    65	      if (skipCardIds.has(cardId)) continue;
    66	      const localCard = localCards.get(cardId);
    67	      if (localCard) copyCardGeometry(localCard, incomingCard);
    68	    }
    69	  }
    70	  if (Array.isArray(incomingLedger.annotations)) {
    71	    for (const incomingAnnotation of incomingLedger.annotations as AnyRecord[]) {
    72	      const annotationId = String(incomingAnnotation.id ?? '');
    73	      if (skipAnnotationIds.has(annotationId)) continue;
    74	      const localAnnotation = localAnnotations.get(annotationId);
    75	      if (localAnnotation) copyAnnotationGeometry(localAnnotation, incomingAnnotation);
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	  return incomingLedger;
    79	}
````

````text
     2	 * WHAT: Commits a ledger mutation and replaces active state with the reconciled server ledger.

    13	export type ActiveLedgerMutation = {
    14	  action: 'create-card' | 'patch-card' | 'delete-card' | 'delete-card-image' | 'create-zone' | 'create-group' | 'create-relationship' | 'delete-zones' | 'delete-relationships' | 'patch-geometry' | 'patch-viewport' | 'patch-region' | 'append-note' | 'update-note' | 'delete-note' | 'paste-selection';
    15	  card?: Record<string, unknown>;
    16	  cardId?: string;
    17	  imageSrc?: string;
    18	  cardPatch?: {
    19	    id: string;
    20	    status?: 'todo' | 'done';
    21	    title?: string;
    22	    description?: string;

    24	  };
    25	  annotation?: Record<string, unknown>;
    26	  relationship?: Record<string, unknown>;
    27	  zoneIds?: string[];
    28	  groupIds?: string[];
    29	  relationshipIds?: string[];
    30	  geometry?: {
    31	    cards?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    32	    zones?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    33	    groups?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    34	  };
    35	  viewport?: { x: number; y: number; scale: number };
    36	  region?: {
    37	    id: string;
    38	    kind: 'zone' | 'group';
    39	    label?: string;
    40	    color?: string;
    41	  };
    42	  note?: {
    43	    id?: string;
    44	    threadId: string;
    45	    body?: string;
    46	    voiceFileRef?: string;
    47	    status?: string;
    48	    transcriptionStartedAt?: string;
    49	    source?: string;
    50	    error?: string;
    51	    imageSizes?: Record<string, { width?: number; height?: number }>;
    52	  };

    54	    cardIds: string[];
    55	    zoneIds: string[];
    56	    groupIds: string[];
    57	  };
    58	};
    59	
    60	function localCanvasMergeOptionsForMutation(mutation: ActiveLedgerMutation): { skipCardIds: Set<string>; skipAnnotationIds: Set<string> } | undefined {
    61	  if (mutation.action !== 'patch-geometry') return undefined;
    62	  return {
    63	    skipCardIds: new Set(Object.keys(mutation.geometry?.cards ?? {})),
    64	    skipAnnotationIds: new Set([
    65	      ...Object.keys(mutation.geometry?.zones ?? {}),
    66	      ...Object.keys(mutation.geometry?.groups ?? {})
    67	    ])
    68	  };
    69	}
    70	
    71	export async function commitActiveLedgerMutation(mutation: ActiveLedgerMutation, options: { render?: boolean } = {}): Promise<boolean> {
    72	  const endpoint = ledgerEndpointForTab(state.activeTab);
    73	  if (!endpoint) return false;
    74	  const ledgerStateId = state.canvasMode === 'ledgers' ? 'ledgers-canvas' : state.activeTab;
    75	  const canMergeLocalCanvas = Boolean(state.activeLedger && state.activeLedgerId === ledgerStateId);
    76	  const localLedger = canMergeLocalCanvas ? state.activeLedger : null;
    77	  const mergeOptions = localCanvasMergeOptionsForMutation(mutation);
    78	  telemetry('commit-ledger-edit', { activeTab: state.activeTab, action: mutation.action, authority: 'server' });
    79	  const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
    80	    method: 'PATCH',
    81	    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    82	    body: JSON.stringify(mutation)
    83	  }).catch(() => undefined);
    84	  if (!response?.ok) {
    85	    telemetry('commit-ledger-edit-failed', { activeTab: state.activeTab, action: mutation.action, authority: 'server' });
    86	    return false;
    87	  }
    88	  const ledger = await response.json().catch(() => null);
    89	  if (!ledger || typeof ledger !== 'object') return false;
    90	  state.activeLedger = mergeLocalThreadNotes(canMergeLocalCanvas ? mergeLocalCanvasStateIntoLedger(ledger, localLedger, mergeOptions) : ledger);
    91	  state.activeLedgerId = ledgerStateId;
    92	  refreshZoneAttributionCache(`server-ledger-mutation:${mutation.action}`);
    93	  telemetry('load-ledger-state', { activeTab: state.activeTab, source: 'server-ledger-mutation', action: mutation.action });
    94	  if (options.render) renderCanvasSurface({ renderThreadPanel: mutation.action !== 'patch-geometry' });
    95	  return true;
    96	}
````

````text
    35	export function resizeZoneGeometryToContainedCards(cards: BoxGeometry[], options: { padding?: number; minWidth?: number; minHeight?: number } = {}): BoxGeometry | null {

   225	  const sourceByCardId = new Map(allCards.map((card) => [card.dataset.cardId ?? '', sourceCardGeometry(card, ledgerCards)]));
   226	  const cardsByZoneId = selectedZoneCardMap(allCards, zones, sourceByCardId);
   227	  const cards = uniqueCards([...selectedCards, ...Array.from(cardsByZoneId.values()).flat()]);
   228	  const detail = clearLowDetailForMeasurement();
   229	  const restoreForcedDetails = forceCardDetailsForMeasurement(cards.map((card) => card.dataset.cardId ?? ''));
   230	  let result: ResizeToContentGeometry = { cards: {}, zones: {} };
   231	  try {
   232	    const measured = cards.map((card) => {
   233	      const sourceGeometry = sourceByCardId.get(card.dataset.cardId ?? '') ?? sourceCardGeometry(card, ledgerCards);
   234	      const height = measureNaturalCardHeight(card, sourceGeometry.width);
   235	      return {
   236	        id: card.dataset.cardId ?? '',
   237	        left: sourceGeometry.x,
   238	        top: sourceGeometry.y,
   239	        width: Math.ceil(sourceGeometry.width),
   240	        height
   241	      };
   242	    });
   243	    const arranged = resolveCardYOverlap(measured);
   244	    const byId = new Map(cards.map((card) => [card.dataset.cardId ?? '', card]));
   245	    const geometry: ResizedCardGeometry = {};
   246	
   247	    for (const record of arranged) {
   248	      const card = byId.get(record.id);
   249	      if (!card) continue;
   250	      applyCardBox(card, { x: record.left, y: record.top, width: record.width, height: record.height });
   251	      geometry[record.id] = { x: record.left, y: record.top, width: record.width, height: record.height };
   252	    }
   253	    const resizedZones = expandSelectedZonesToCards(cardsByZoneId, zones, sourceByCardId, geometry);
   254	    result = { cards: geometry, zones: resizedZones };
   255	  } finally {
   256	    restoreDetailClasses(detail);
   257	    restoreForcedDetails();
   258	  }
   259	  renderRelationshipOverlay();
   260	  if (Object.keys(result.zones).length > 0) renderZoneLabelOverlay();
   261	  telemetry('resize-selected-cards', { count: Object.keys(result.cards).length, cardIds: Object.keys(result.cards), zoneIds: Object.keys(result.zones) });
   262	  return result;
   263	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Applies pointer resize deltas to a selected zone or group and its ledger annotation.
     3	 * WHY: Region resizing must survive canvas remounts without losing target identity or variant limits.
     4	 */
     5	import { state } from '../../state.js';
     6	import { renderCanvasControlOverlay } from '../../canvas/effect/render-canvas-control-overlay.js';
     7	import { renderZoneLabelOverlay } from './render-zone-label-overlay.js';
     8	import { telemetry } from '../../telemetry/effect/telemetry.js';
     9	import { activeLedgerAnnotationMap, ledgerAnnotationGeometry, patchLedgerAnnotationGeometry, type LedgerGeometry } from '../../ledger/helper/active-ledger-geometry.js';
    10	import { renderGeometry } from '../../canvas/helper/render-density.js';
    11	import { resolveCurrentPointerTarget } from '../../gesture/helper/resolve-current-pointer-target.js';
    12	
    13	export function resizeSelectedZone(dx: number, dy: number): void {
    14	  const pointer = state.pointer;
    15	  const savedZone = pointer?.target as HTMLElement | null;
    16	  const targetKind = pointer?.targetKind === 'group' ? 'group' : 'zone';
    17	  const id = String(pointer?.targetId || savedZone?.dataset.zoneId || savedZone?.dataset.groupId || '');
    18	  const zone = resolveCurrentPointerTarget(targetKind, id, savedZone);
    19	  const ledgerAnnotation = state.activeLedger && id ? activeLedgerAnnotationMap().get(id) : undefined;
    20	  // WHAT: Stop only when neither persisted annotation geometry nor a live target remains.
    21	  // WHY: A remounted canvas can invalidate either representation independently.
    22	  if (!zone && !ledgerAnnotation) return;
    23	  const isGroup = targetKind === 'group' || zone?.dataset.groupId === id || ledgerAnnotation?.variant === 'group';
    24	  const current = ledgerAnnotation
    25	    ? ledgerAnnotationGeometry(ledgerAnnotation)
    26	    : { x: zone?.offsetLeft ?? 0, y: zone?.offsetTop ?? 0, width: zone?.offsetWidth ?? 0, height: zone?.offsetHeight ?? 0 };
    27	  const handle = pointer?.resizeHandle as HTMLElement | null;
    28	  const west = Boolean(handle?.classList.contains('nw') || handle?.classList.contains('sw'));
    29	  const east = Boolean(handle?.classList.contains('ne') || handle?.classList.contains('se'));
    30	  const north = Boolean(handle?.classList.contains('nw') || handle?.classList.contains('ne'));
    31	  const south = Boolean(handle?.classList.contains('sw') || handle?.classList.contains('se'));
    32	  const minWidth = isGroup ? 220 : 180;
    33	  const minHeight = isGroup ? 160 : 140;
    34	  let nextLeft = current.x;
    35	  let nextTop = current.y;
    36	  let nextWidth = current.width;
    37	  let nextHeight = current.height;
    38	  // WHAT: Clamp west and north movement while preserving the opposite edge.
    39	  // WHY: Region resizing must honor variant minimums without shifting the anchored edge.
    40	  if (west) {
    41	    const clampedDx = Math.min(dx, current.width - minWidth);
    42	    nextLeft = current.x + clampedDx;
    43	    nextWidth = current.width - clampedDx;
    44	  }
    45	  if (east) nextWidth = Math.max(minWidth, current.width + dx);
    46	  if (north) {
    47	    const clampedDy = Math.min(dy, current.height - minHeight);
    48	    nextTop = current.y + clampedDy;
    49	    nextHeight = current.height - clampedDy;
    50	  }
    51	  if (south) nextHeight = Math.max(minHeight, current.height + dy);
    52	  const geometry = { x: nextLeft, y: nextTop, width: nextWidth, height: nextHeight };
    53	  if (ledgerAnnotation) patchLedgerAnnotationGeometry(ledgerAnnotation, geometry);
    54	  if (zone) patchZoneBox(zone, geometry);
    55	  renderZoneLabelOverlay();
    56	  renderCanvasControlOverlay();
    57	  telemetry(isGroup ? 'render-group-layer' : 'render-zone-layer', { resized: id, geometry });
    58	}
    59	
    60	function patchZoneBox(zone: HTMLElement, geometry: LedgerGeometry): void {
    61	  const renderedGeometry = state.activeLedger ? renderGeometry(geometry) : geometry;
    62	  zone.style.left = `${renderedGeometry.x}px`;
    63	  zone.style.top = `${renderedGeometry.y}px`;
    64	  zone.style.width = `${renderedGeometry.width}px`;
    65	  zone.style.height = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    66	  zone.style.minHeight = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    67	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Starts canvas pointer gestures and resolves their target intent.
     3	 * WHY: Drag, pan, resize, draw, and edit control flow must share one canonical pointer entrypoint.
     4	 */
     5	import { canvas } from '../../dom.js';
     6	import { state } from '../../state.js';
     7	import { derivePointerIntent, isForcedPanPointer } from '../helper/derive-pointer-intent.js';
     8	import { canvasPoint } from '../../canvas/helper/canvas-point.js';
     9	import { patchBox } from '../../canvas/effect/patch-box.js';
    10	import { isGestureControlTarget } from '../helper/is-gesture-control-target.js';
    11	import { point } from '../helper/point.js';
    12	import { shouldPreservePointerSelection } from '../../selection/helper/should-preserve-pointer-selection.js';
    13	import { selectTarget } from '../../selection/controller/select-target.js';
    14	import { renderSelectionState } from '../../selection/effect/render-selection-state.js';
    15	import { selectThread } from '../../thread/effect/select-thread.js';
    16	import { closeThreadPanel } from '../../thread/effect/close-thread-panel.js';
    17	import { telemetry } from '../../telemetry/effect/telemetry.js';
    18	import { createPointerSelectionSnapshot } from '../helper/create-pointer-selection-snapshot.js';
    19	import { currentLedgerStateId } from '../../ledger/helper/current-ledger-state-id.js';
    20	
    21	export function handlePointerDown(event: PointerEvent): void {
    22	  const rawTarget = event.target as HTMLElement;
    23	  const forcedPan = isForcedPanPointer(event);
    24	  if (!forcedPan && isGestureControlTarget(rawTarget)) return;
    25	  const now = performance.now();
    26	  event.preventDefault();
    27	  const resizeHandle = rawTarget.closest('.resize-handle') as HTMLElement | null;
    28	  const target = rawTarget.closest('[data-card-id],[data-zone-id],[data-group-id]') as HTMLElement | null;
    29	  const targetKind = target?.dataset.cardId ? 'card' : target?.dataset.groupId ? 'group' : target?.dataset.zoneId ? 'zone' : 'canvas';
    30	  const targetId = target?.dataset.cardId ?? target?.dataset.groupId ?? target?.dataset.zoneId ?? '';
    31	  const pointer = point(event);
    32	  const canvasPointer = canvasPoint(pointer);
    33	  const intent = derivePointerIntent(event, targetKind, resizeHandle);
    34	  state.pointer = { intent, resizeHandle, target, targetKind, targetId, start: pointer, current: pointer, startCanvas: canvasPointer, currentCanvas: canvasPointer, startedAt: now, ctrlPan: event.ctrlKey, forcedPan };
    35	  telemetry('canvas-pointer-down', { intent, targetKind, targetId, ctrlKey: event.ctrlKey, middleButton: event.button === 1, shiftKey: event.shiftKey });
    36	  telemetry('derive-gesture-intent', { kind: intent });
    37	  if (intent === 'pan' && targetKind === 'canvas' && !forcedPan) {
    38	    state.selection = { cardIds: [], zoneIds: [], groupIds: [] };
    39	    selectThread('');
    40	    if (state.threadPanelOpen || state.activeTool === 'thread') closeThreadPanel();
    41	    (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null)?.blur?.();
    42	    telemetry('clear-transient-selection', { reason: 'canvas-background-pointer-down' });
    43	    renderSelectionState();
    44	  }
    45	  const preserveSelection = shouldPreservePointerSelection(state.selection, targetKind, targetId, event.shiftKey);
    46	  if ((intent === 'drag' || intent === 'group') && !preserveSelection) selectTarget(targetKind, targetId, event.shiftKey);
    47	  if (intent === 'resize') selectTarget(targetKind, targetId, false);
    48	  if (state.pointer && (intent === 'drag' || intent === 'group' || intent === 'resize')) {
    49	    // WHAT: Freeze gesture identity after pointer-down selection has settled.
    50	    // WHY: Async refresh and later selection changes must not redirect movement or commit payloads.
    51	    state.pointer.selectionSnapshot = createPointerSelectionSnapshot({
    52	      selection: state.selection,
    53	      targetKind,
    54	      targetId,
    55	      ledgerStateId: currentLedgerStateId()
    56	    });
    57	  }
    58	  if (intent === 'marquee' || intent === 'draw-card' || intent === 'draw-zone' || intent === 'draw-group') {
    59	    const marquee = document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement;
    60	    marquee.hidden = false;
    61	    patchBox(marquee, canvasPointer.x, canvasPointer.y, 0, 0);
    62	  }
    63	  canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
    64	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Moves one explicit selection through ledger geometry or static DOM geometry.
     3	 * WHY: Active gestures must keep using their pointer-down selection across refresh and live selection changes.
     4	 */
     5	import { state, type SelectionState } from '../../state.js';
     6	import { cloneSelectionState } from '../helper/clone-selection-state.js';
     7	import { renderCanvasControlOverlay } from '../../canvas/effect/render-canvas-control-overlay.js';
     8	import { renderRelationshipOverlay } from '../../relationship/effect/render-relationship-overlay.js';
     9	import { renderZoneLabelOverlay } from '../../zone/effect/render-zone-label-overlay.js';

    12	import { renderGeometry } from '../../canvas/helper/render-density.js';
    13	
    14	export function moveSelected(dx: number, dy: number, selection: Partial<SelectionState> = state.selection): void {
    15	  const movingSelection = cloneSelectionState(selection);
    16	  // WHAT: Patch the authoritative ledger when present; otherwise update static canvas nodes.
    17	  // WHY: Both canvas modes share gesture control flow but own geometry in different stores.
    18	  if (state.activeLedger) {
    19	    moveSelectedLedgerGeometry(dx, dy, movingSelection);
    20	  } else {
    21	    moveSelectedDomGeometry(dx, dy, movingSelection);
    22	  }
    23	  telemetry('render-card-layer', { moved: movingSelection.cardIds });
    24	  telemetry('render-zone-layer', { moved: movingSelection.zoneIds });
    25	  telemetry('render-group-layer', { moved: movingSelection.groupIds });
    26	  renderZoneLabelOverlay();
    27	  renderRelationshipOverlay();
    28	  renderCanvasControlOverlay(movingSelection);
    29	}
    30	
    31	function moveSelectedLedgerGeometry(dx: number, dy: number, selection: SelectionState): void {
    32	  const cards = activeLedgerCardMap();
    33	  const annotations = activeLedgerAnnotationMap();
    34	  for (const id of selection.cardIds) {
    35	    const card = cards.get(id);
    36	    if (!card) continue;
    37	    const geometry = ledgerCardGeometry(card);
    38	    patchLedgerCardGeometry(card, { ...geometry, x: geometry.x + dx, y: geometry.y + dy });
    39	    patchNodePosition(document.querySelector(`[data-card-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`) as HTMLElement | null, geometry.x + dx, geometry.y + dy);
    40	  }
    41	  for (const id of selection.zoneIds) {
    42	    const annotation = annotations.get(id);
    43	    if (!annotation) continue;
    44	    const geometry = ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
    45	    patchLedgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation, { ...geometry, x: geometry.x + dx, y: geometry.y + dy });
    46	    patchNodePosition(document.querySelector(`[data-zone-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`) as HTMLElement | null, geometry.x + dx, geometry.y + dy);
    47	  }
    48	  for (const id of selection.groupIds) {
    49	    const annotation = annotations.get(id);
    50	    if (!annotation) continue;
    51	    const geometry = ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
    52	    patchLedgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation, { ...geometry, x: geometry.x + dx, y: geometry.y + dy });
    53	    patchNodePosition(document.querySelector(`[data-group-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`) as HTMLElement | null, geometry.x + dx, geometry.y + dy);
    54	  }
    55	}
    56	
    57	function moveSelectedDomGeometry(dx: number, dy: number, selection: SelectionState): void {
````

````text
     7	import { createCardController } from '../../card/controller/create-card-controller.js';

    20	import { resizeSelectedZone } from '../../zone/effect/resize-selected-zone.js';
    21	import { isClickMovement, pointerDistancePx } from '../helper/click-movement.js';
    22	import { telemetry } from '../../telemetry/effect/telemetry.js';
    23	
    24	export async function handlePointerUp(event: PointerEvent): Promise<void> {
    25	  if (!state.pointer) return;
    26	  event.preventDefault();
    27	  const pointerSession = state.pointer;
    28	  const pointerIntent = pointerSession.intent;
    29	  let releaseRendered = false;
    30	  telemetry('canvas-pointer-up', { intent: pointerIntent });
    31	  const releasePoint = point(event);
    32	  const releaseCanvas = canvasPoint(releasePoint);
    33	  const moved = pointerDistancePx(pointerSession.start, releasePoint);
    34	  const isForcedPan = Boolean(pointerSession.forcedPan || pointerSession.ctrlPan);
    35	  if (!isForcedPan && pointerIntent === 'pan' && pointerSession.targetKind === 'zone' && isClickMovement(moved)) {
    36	    selectTarget('zone', pointerSession.targetId, false);
    37	    telemetry('resolve-selection-target', { kind: 'zone', id: pointerSession.targetId, clickSelect: true });
    38	  }
    39	  if (!isForcedPan && pointerIntent === 'pan' && pointerSession.targetKind === 'group' && isClickMovement(moved)) {
    40	    selectTarget('group', pointerSession.targetId, false);
    41	    telemetry('resolve-selection-target', { kind: 'group', id: pointerSession.targetId, clickSelect: true });
    42	  }
    43	  if (pointerIntent === 'marquee') {
    44	    const rect = rectFromPoints(pointerSession.startCanvas, releaseCanvas);
    45	    selectIntersecting(rect);
    46	    (document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement).hidden = true;
    47	    telemetry('resolve-selection-target', { selection: state.selection });
    48	  }
    49	  if (pointerIntent === 'draw-card') {
    50	    const rect = rectFromPoints(pointerSession.startCanvas, releaseCanvas);
    51	    (document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement).hidden = true;
    52	    finishPointer(event);
    53	    await createCardController(rect);
    54	  }
    55	  if (pointerIntent === 'draw-zone') {
    56	    const rect = rectFromPoints(pointerSession.startCanvas, releaseCanvas);
    57	    (document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement).hidden = true;
    58	    finishPointer(event);
    59	    await createZoneController(rect);
    60	  }
    61	  if (pointerIntent === 'draw-group') {
    62	    const rect = rectFromPoints(pointerSession.startCanvas, releaseCanvas);
    63	    (document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement).hidden = true;
    64	    finishPointer(event);
    65	    await createGroupController(rect);
    66	  }
    67	  if ((pointerIntent === 'drag' || pointerIntent === 'group' || pointerIntent === 'resize') && isClickMovement(moved)) {
    68	    finishPointer(event);
    69	    persistState();
    70	    return;
    71	  }
    72	  if (pointerIntent === 'drag' || pointerIntent === 'group' || pointerIntent === 'resize') {
    73	    const gestureSelection = pointerSession.selectionSnapshot ?? state.selection;
    74	    const canvasDx = releaseCanvas.x - pointerSession.currentCanvas.x;
    75	    const canvasDy = releaseCanvas.y - pointerSession.currentCanvas.y;
    76	    if (canvasDx || canvasDy) {
    77	      if (pointerIntent === 'drag' || pointerIntent === 'group') moveSelected(canvasDx, canvasDy, gestureSelection);
    78	      if (pointerIntent === 'resize') {
    79	        if (pointerSession.targetKind === 'card') resizeSelectedCard(canvasDx, canvasDy);
    80	        else resizeSelectedZone(canvasDx, canvasDy);
    81	      }
    82	    }
    83	    finishPointer(event);
    84	    releaseRendered = await commitSelectedLedgerGeometry(gestureSelection);
    85	  }
    86	  if (pointerIntent === 'pan' || pointerIntent === 'marquee') finishPointer(event);
    87	  persistState();
    88	  if (pointerIntent !== 'pan' && !releaseRendered) renderCanvasSurface();
    89	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Applies active pointer gesture movement to the canvas runtime.
     3	 * WHY: Pan must stay transform-only while drag, resize, and draw paths update their owned geometry.
     4	 */
     5	import { state } from '../../state.js';
     6	import { applyViewportTransform } from '../../canvas/effect/apply-viewport-transform.js';
     7	import { applyPanViewportTransform } from '../../canvas/effect/apply-pan-viewport-transform.js';
     8	import { canvasPoint } from '../../canvas/helper/canvas-point.js';
     9	import { moveSelected } from '../../selection/effect/move-selected.js';
    10	import { patchBox } from '../../canvas/effect/patch-box.js';
    11	import { point } from '../helper/point.js';
    12	import { rectFromPoints } from '../../canvas/helper/rect-from-points.js';
    13	import { resizeSelectedCard } from '../../card/effect/resize-selected-card.js';
    14	import { resizeSelectedZone } from '../../zone/effect/resize-selected-zone.js';
    15	import { emitPanPerformanceTelemetry } from '../effect/emit-pan-performance-telemetry.js';
    16	import { schedulePanningEffects } from '../effect/schedule-panning-effects.js';
    17	import { isClickMovement, pointerDistancePx } from '../helper/click-movement.js';
    18	import { telemetry } from '../../telemetry/effect/telemetry.js';
    19	
    20	export function handlePointerMove(event: PointerEvent): void {
    21	  if (!state.pointer) return;
    22	  event.preventDefault();
    23	  const pointer = point(event);
    24	  const dx = pointer.x - state.pointer.current.x;
    25	  const dy = pointer.y - state.pointer.current.y;
    26	  const isPan = state.pointer.intent === 'pan';
    27	  const isGeometryGesture = state.pointer.intent === 'drag' || state.pointer.intent === 'group' || state.pointer.intent === 'resize';
    28	  if (isGeometryGesture && isClickMovement(pointerDistancePx(state.pointer.start, pointer))) return;
    29	  const canvasPointer = isPan ? state.pointer.currentCanvas : canvasPoint(pointer);
    30	  const canvasDx = isPan ? 0 : canvasPointer.x - state.pointer.currentCanvas.x;
    31	  const canvasDy = isPan ? 0 : canvasPointer.y - state.pointer.currentCanvas.y;
    32	  state.pointer.current = pointer;
    33	  state.pointer.currentCanvas = canvasPointer;
    34	  if (isPan) {
    35	    const frameStartedAt = performance.now();
    36	    state.viewport.x += dx;
    37	    state.viewport.y += dy;
    38	    applyPanViewportTransform();
    39	    schedulePanningEffects();
    40	    emitPanPerformanceTelemetry({ dx, dy, durationMs: performance.now() - frameStartedAt, frameStartedAt });
    41	    return;
    42	  }
    43	  telemetry('canvas-pointer-move', { intent: state.pointer.intent, dx, dy, canvasDx, canvasDy });
    44	  if (state.pointer.intent === 'drag' || state.pointer.intent === 'group') {
    45	    moveSelected(canvasDx, canvasDy, state.pointer.selectionSnapshot ?? state.selection);
    46	    telemetry('calculate-drag-delta', { dx, dy, canvasDx, canvasDy });
    47	  }
    48	  if (state.pointer.intent === 'resize') {
    49	    if (state.pointer.targetKind === 'card') resizeSelectedCard(canvasDx, canvasDy);
    50	    else resizeSelectedZone(canvasDx, canvasDy);
    51	    telemetry('calculate-drag-delta', { dx, dy, canvasDx, canvasDy, resizeHandle: state.pointer.resizeHandle?.className });
    52	  }
    53	  if (state.pointer.intent === 'marquee' || state.pointer.intent === 'draw-card' || state.pointer.intent === 'draw-zone' || state.pointer.intent === 'draw-group') {
    54	    const rect = rectFromPoints(state.pointer.startCanvas, canvasPointer);
    55	    patchBox(document.querySelector('.marquee') as HTMLElement, rect.x, rect.y, rect.width, rect.height);
    56	    telemetry(state.pointer.intent === 'marquee' ? 'calculate-marquee-selection' : 'calculate-draft-geometry', { intent: state.pointer.intent, rect });
    57	  }
    58	}
````

````text
    76	  const done = (): void => {
    77	    pending -= 1;
    78	    if (pending === 0) callback();
    79	  };
    80	  for (const stream of [stdout, stderr]) {

   247	    const detail = status === 'cancelled' ? 'terminated by operator' : `exit code ${exitCode ?? 'unknown'}`;
   248	    appendRunStatus(runSummaryFile, status, detail);
   249	    updateRuntimeRun(runtime, runId, { status, exitCode, finishedAt });
   250	    finishRunStreams(stdout, stderr, () => {
   251	      if (status === 'cancelled') appendFileSync(stderrFile, `Codex run cancelled: ${detail}\n`, 'utf8');
   252	      void readCardSkillRunController({ action_payload: { ledgerId, cardId, runId }, runtime_state: runtime })
   253	        .catch(() => undefined)
   254	        .finally(() => {
   255	          notifyLedgerChange(payload.onLedgerChange, { reason: status === 'cancelled' ? 'codex-thread-cancelled' : 'codex-thread-finished', ledgerId, cardId, threadId, runId, exitCode });
   256	          notifyRunSettled(runtime.onCodexRunSettled, { ledgerId, cardId, threadId, runId, status, exitCode });
   257	        });
   258	    });
   259	  });
   260	
   261	  return { ok: true, statusCode: 202, run: publicRun(run) };
   262	}
````

````text
   215	export function resizeSelectedCardsToContent(target: ResizeToContentTarget = {}): ResizeToContentGeometry {
   216	  const selectedCards = selectedCardElements(target);
   217	  const zones = selectedZoneElements(target);
   218	  if (selectedCards.length === 0 && zones.length === 0) {
   219	    telemetry('resize-selected-cards', { count: 0 });
   220	    return { cards: {}, zones: {} };
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  const ledgerCards = activeLedgerCardMap();
   224	  const allCards = allCardElements();
   225	  const sourceByCardId = new Map(allCards.map((card) => [card.dataset.cardId ?? '', sourceCardGeometry(card, ledgerCards)]));
   226	  const cardsByZoneId = selectedZoneCardMap(allCards, zones, sourceByCardId);
   227	  const cards = uniqueCards([...selectedCards, ...Array.from(cardsByZoneId.values()).flat()]);
   228	  const detail = clearLowDetailForMeasurement();
   229	  const restoreForcedDetails = forceCardDetailsForMeasurement(cards.map((card) => card.dataset.cardId ?? ''));
   230	  let result: ResizeToContentGeometry = { cards: {}, zones: {} };
   231	  try {
   232	    const measured = cards.map((card) => {
   233	      const sourceGeometry = sourceByCardId.get(card.dataset.cardId ?? '') ?? sourceCardGeometry(card, ledgerCards);
   234	      const height = measureNaturalCardHeight(card, sourceGeometry.width);
   235	      return {
   236	        id: card.dataset.cardId ?? '',
   237	        left: sourceGeometry.x,
   238	        top: sourceGeometry.y,
   239	        width: Math.ceil(sourceGeometry.width),
   240	        height
   241	      };
   242	    });
   243	    const arranged = resolveCardYOverlap(measured);
   244	    const byId = new Map(cards.map((card) => [card.dataset.cardId ?? '', card]));
   245	    const geometry: ResizedCardGeometry = {};
   246	
   247	    for (const record of arranged) {
   248	      const card = byId.get(record.id);
   249	      if (!card) continue;
   250	      applyCardBox(card, { x: record.left, y: record.top, width: record.width, height: record.height });
   251	      geometry[record.id] = { x: record.left, y: record.top, width: record.width, height: record.height };
   252	    }
   253	    const resizedZones = expandSelectedZonesToCards(cardsByZoneId, zones, sourceByCardId, geometry);
   254	    result = { cards: geometry, zones: resizedZones };
   255	  } finally {
   256	    restoreDetailClasses(detail);
   257	    restoreForcedDetails();
   258	  }
   259	  renderRelationshipOverlay();
   260	  if (Object.keys(result.zones).length > 0) renderZoneLabelOverlay();
   261	  telemetry('resize-selected-cards', { count: Object.keys(result.cards).length, cardIds: Object.keys(result.cards), zoneIds: Object.keys(result.zones) });
   262	  return result;
   263	}
````

````text
   226	
   227	function elapsedMs(input: { runtime: AnyRecord; runId: string; status: RunStatus; stdoutFile: string; stderrFile: string }): number {
   228	  const runs = input.runtime.codexSkillRuns && typeof input.runtime.codexSkillRuns === 'object' ? input.runtime.codexSkillRuns as Record<string, AnyRecord> : {};
   229	  const run = runs[input.runId] ?? {};
   230	  const started = runSegmentStartedAtMs({ runtime: input.runtime, runId: input.runId, stderrFile: input.stderrFile });
   231	  const finished = Date.parse(String(run.finishedAt ?? ''));
   232	  const terminalFileWrite = Math.max(fileMtimeMs(input.stdoutFile), fileMtimeMs(input.stderrFile));
   233	  const end = finished || (input.status === 'running' ? Date.now() : terminalFileWrite || Date.now());
   234	  return Math.max(0, end - started);
   235	}
   236	
   237	function cardReferencesRun(input: { ledger: AnyRecord; decisionOsRoot: string; cardId: string; runId: string }): boolean {
   238	  const hydrated = hydrateLedgerCardContent(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(input.ledger)), input.decisionOsRoot) as { cards?: AnyRecord[] };
   239	  const card = (hydrated.cards ?? []).find((entry) => String(entry.id ?? '') === input.cardId);
   240	  if (!card) return false;
   241	  if (String(card.codexThreadRunId ?? '') === input.runId || String(card.codexRunId ?? '') === input.runId) return true;
   242	  if (String(card.cardType ?? '') === 'codex-skill-run' && input.cardId === `card-${safeSegment(input.runId)}`) return true;
   243	  const comment = card?.comment && typeof card.comment === 'object' ? card.comment as AnyRecord : {};
   244	  const body = String(comment.what ?? comment.body ?? comment.description ?? '');
   245	  return body.includes(`Codex run: ${input.runId}`);
   246	}
   247	
   248	function persistRunEvents(input: { decisionOsRoot: string; ledgerPath: string; ledger: AnyRecord; cardId: string; runId: string; events: NormalizedRunEvent[] }): number {
   249	  hydrateLedgerThreadNotes(input.ledger, input.decisionOsRoot);
   250	  const threadId = `thread-${input.cardId}`;
   251	  const notesByThread = normalizeLedgerNotes(input.ledger);
   252	  const notes = notesByThread[threadId] ?? [];
   253	  const byId = new Map(notes.map((note) => [String(note.id ?? ''), note]));
   254	  let changed = 0;
   255	  for (const event of input.events) {
   256	    if (!event.persist) continue;
   257	    const id = `codex-${safeSegment(input.runId)}-line-${event.line}`;
   258	    const existing = byId.get(id);
   259	    const nextNote: AnyRecord = {
   260	      id,
   261	      role: 'agent',
   262	      message: event.text || event.title,
   263	      timestamp: String(existing?.timestamp ?? '') || new Date().toISOString(),
   264	      status: event.status || event.title,
   265	      codexRunId: input.runId,
   266	      codexLine: String(event.line),
   267	      codexKind: event.kind,
   268	      codexEventType: event.type,
   269	      codexItemId: event.itemId,
   270	      codexTool: event.tool,
   271	      codexExitCode: event.exitCode,
   272	    };
   273	    if (existing) {
   274	      const previous = JSON.stringify(existing);
   275	      Object.assign(existing, nextNote);
   276	      if (JSON.stringify(existing) !== previous) changed += 1;
   277	    } else {
   278	      const insertAt = notes.findIndex((note) => String(note.codexRunId ?? '') === input.runId && noteCodexLine(note) > event.line);
   279	      if (insertAt >= 0) notes.splice(insertAt, 0, nextNote);
   280	      else notes.push(nextNote);
   281	      byId.set(id, nextNote);
   282	      changed += 1;
   283	    }
   284	  }
   285	  if (changed > 0) {
   286	    notesByThread[threadId] = notes;
   287	    writeThreadNotesFile({ decisionOsRoot: input.decisionOsRoot, ledger: input.ledger, ledgerPath: input.ledgerPath, threadId, notes });
   288	    stripHydratedThreadNotes(input.ledger);
   289	    writeFileSync(input.ledgerPath, JSON.stringify(input.ledger, null, 2), 'utf8');
   290	  }
   291	  return changed;
   292	}
   293	
   294	export async function readCardSkillRunController(input: { action_payload?: AnyRecord; runtime_state?: AnyRecord; data_model?: AnyRecord } | AnyRecord = {}): Promise<AnyRecord> {
   295	  const envelope = input as { action_payload?: AnyRecord; runtime_state?: AnyRecord; data_model?: AnyRecord };
   296	  const payload = (envelope.action_payload ?? input) as AnyRecord;
   297	  const runtime = (envelope.runtime_state ?? {}) as AnyRecord;
   298	  const decisionOsRoot = resolve(String(runtime.decisionOsRoot ?? resolve(process.cwd(), '.decision-os')));
   299	  const ledgerId = String(payload.ledgerId ?? '').trim();
   300	  const cardId = String(payload.cardId ?? '').trim();
   301	  const runId = String(payload.runId ?? '').trim();
   302	  const since = Math.max(0, Number(payload.since ?? 0) || 0);
   303	  const traceId = String(payload.traceId ?? '');
   304	  logCodexContinueDebug('read-controller-entry', { traceId, ledgerId, cardId, runId, since });
   305	  if (!ledgerId || !cardId || !runId) return { ok: false, statusCode: 400, error: 'Missing ledgerId, cardId, or runId.' };
   306	
   307	  const state = readCanonicalDecisionOsState({ action_payload: { decisionOsFile: resolve(decisionOsRoot, 'state.json') }, runtime_state: runtime });
   308	  const tab = state.ledgers.find((entry) => entry.id === ledgerId);
   309	  if (!tab) return { ok: false, statusCode: 404, error: 'Ledger not found.', ledgerId };
   310	
   311	  const ledgerFile = String(tab.ledgerFile ?? '').replace(/^\.decision-os\//, '');
   312	  const ledgerPath = resolve(decisionOsRoot, ledgerFile);
   313	  if (!isInside(decisionOsRoot, ledgerPath) || !existsSync(ledgerPath)) return { ok: false, statusCode: 404, error: 'Ledger file not found.', ledgerId };
   314	
   315	  const ledger = JSON.parse(readFileSync(ledgerPath, 'utf8')) as AnyRecord & { cards?: AnyRecord[] };
   316	  if (!cardReferencesRun({ ledger, decisionOsRoot, cardId, runId })) return { ok: false, statusCode: 404, error: 'Run not found on card.', cardId, runId };
   317	
   318	  const runDirectory = resolve(decisionOsRoot, 'runs', 'codex-skills', safeSegment(ledgerStem(ledgerPath)));
   319	  const stdoutFile = resolve(runDirectory, `${safeSegment(runId)}.jsonl`);
   320	  const stderrFile = resolve(runDirectory, `${safeSegment(runId)}.log`);
   321	  const stderrLog = existsSync(stderrFile) ? readFileSync(stderrFile, 'utf8') : '';
   322	  const parsedLines = readJsonlLines(stdoutFile);
   323	  const events = parsedLines.map(normalizeRunEvent);
   324	  const segmentStartLine = latestCodexRunSegmentStartLine({ log: stderrLog, runId });
   325	  const segmentEvents = events.filter((event) => event.line > segmentStartLine);
   326	  const segmentLog = latestCodexRunSegmentLog({ log: stderrLog, runId });
   327	  const status = inferredStatus({ runtime, runId, events: segmentEvents, stdoutFile, stderrFile, stderrLog: segmentLog });
   328	  const persistedEventCount = persistRunEvents({ decisionOsRoot, ledgerPath, ledger, cardId, runId, events });
   329	  const returnedEvents = segmentEvents.filter((event) => event.line > since);
   330	  const metadata = { ...runtimeRunMetadata(runtime, runId), ...codexRunSegmentMetadata({ log: stderrLog, runId }) };
   331	  logCodexContinueDebug('read-controller-result', {
   332	    traceId,
   333	    ledgerId,
   334	    cardId,
   335	    runId,
   336	    since,
   337	    status,
   338	    parsedLineCount: parsedLines.length,
   339	    segmentStartLine,
   340	    segmentEventCount: segmentEvents.length,
   341	    lineCount: parsedLines.at(-1)?.line ?? 0,
   342	    returnedEventCount: returnedEvents.length,
   343	    persistedEventCount,
   344	    metadata,
   345	    latestEventType: segmentEvents.at(-1)?.type ?? '',
   346	    latestEventLine: segmentEvents.at(-1)?.line ?? 0,
   347	    stdoutFile,
   348	    stderrFile,
   349	  });
   350	  return {
   351	    ok: true,
   352	    statusCode: 200,
   353	    ledgerId,
   354	    cardId,
   355	    runId,
   356	    status,
   357	    startedAt: new Date(runSegmentStartedAtMs({ runtime, runId, stderrFile })).toISOString(),
   358	    elapsedMs: elapsedMs({ runtime, runId, status, stdoutFile, stderrFile }),
   359	    lineCount: parsedLines.at(-1)?.line ?? 0,
   360	    nextSince: parsedLines.at(-1)?.line ?? 0,
   361	    toolCallCount: segmentEvents.filter((event) => event.kind === 'tool_call' && event.type === 'item.completed').length,
   362	    agentMessageCount: segmentEvents.filter((event) => event.kind === 'agent_message').length,
   363	    fileChangeCount: segmentEvents.filter((event) => event.kind === 'file_change').length,
   364	    thinkingCount: segmentEvents.filter((event) => event.kind === 'thinking').length,
   365	    persistedEventCount,
   366	    metadata,
   367	    latestEvent: segmentEvents.at(-1) ?? null,
   368	    events: returnedEvents,
   369	  };
   370	}
````

````text
    29	export async function handleKeyboard(event: KeyboardEvent): Promise<void> {
    30	  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null;
    31	  const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
    32	  const editableTarget = target?.closest('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable="true"]');
    33	  if (modal.open) {
    34	    if (key === 'enter') {
    35	      event.preventDefault();
    36	      if (modal.dataset.confirmKind === 'note') {
    37	        await deleteNoteController({ threadId: modal.dataset.threadId ?? state.threadId, noteId: modal.dataset.noteId ?? '' });
    38	      } else if (modal.dataset.confirmKind === 'card-image') {
    39	        await deleteCardImageController({
    40	          cardId: modal.dataset.cardId ?? '',
    41	          imageSrc: modal.dataset.imageSrc ?? '',
    42	          carouselSources: modal.dataset.carouselSources,
    43	          carouselSlideIndex: modal.dataset.carouselSlideIndex
    44	        });
    45	      } else if (modal.dataset.confirmKind === 'card') {
    46	        await deleteCardController({ cardId: modal.dataset.cardId ?? '' });
    47	      } else if (modal.dataset.confirmKind === 'group') {
    48	        await deleteGroupController({ groupId: modal.dataset.groupId ?? '' });
    49	      } else {
    50	        await deleteZoneController();
    51	      }
    52	      return;
    53	    }
    54	    if (key === 'escape') {
    55	      event.preventDefault();
    56	      modal.close?.();
    57	      return;
    58	    }
    59	  }
    60	  if (target?.closest('.thread-draft') && event.ctrlKey && key === 'enter') {
    61	    event.preventDefault();
    62	    await submitThreadDraft();
    63	    return;
    64	  }
    65	  if (isCardEditingKeyboardTarget(target)) return;
    66	  if (editableTarget && key !== 'escape') return;
    67	  telemetry('keyboard-shortcut', { key, ctrlKey: event.ctrlKey });
    68	  if (key === 'a') {
    69	    event.preventDefault();
    70	    if (state.threadPanelOpen) focusThreadDraft();
    71	    else openThreadPanel();
    72	    return;
    73	  }
    74	  if (key === 'x') {
    75	    event.preventDefault();
    76	    if (!state.threadPanelOpen) openThreadPanel();
    77	    if (state.voice.recording) await stopVoiceRecording({ queueCodex: event.shiftKey });
    78	    else void startVoiceRecording();
    79	    return;
    80	  }
    81	  if (key === 'escape') {
    82	    if (state.voice.recording) {
    83	      cancelVoiceRecording();
    84	      return;
    85	    }
    86	    if (state.threadPanelOpen || state.activeTool === 'thread') {
    87	      closeThreadPanel();
    88	      return;
    89	    }
    90	    state.selection = { cardIds: [], zoneIds: [], groupIds: [] };
    91	    resetActiveTool('escape');
    92	    telemetry('clear-transient-selection', { reason: 'escape' });
    93	    renderCanvasSurface();
    94	  }
    95	  if (key === 'delete' && state.selection.groupIds.length > 0) {
    96	    confirmGroupDeletionController();
    97	    return;
    98	  }
    99	  if (key === 'delete' && state.selection.cardIds.length > 0) {
   100	    confirmCardDeletionController({ cardId: state.selection.cardIds.at(-1) ?? '' });
   101	    return;
   102	  }
   103	  if (key === 'delete' && state.selection.zoneIds.length > 0) {
   104	    confirmZoneDeletionController();
   105	  }
   106	  if (event.ctrlKey && key === 'c') {
   107	    state.clipboard = structuredClone(state.selection);
   108	    telemetry('copy-selection-payload', state.clipboard);
   109	  }
   110	  if (event.ctrlKey && key === 'd') {
   111	    event.preventDefault();
   112	    await resizeSelectedCardsController();
   113	  }
   114	  if (event.ctrlKey && key === 'v' && state.clipboard) {
   115	    await pasteSelectionController();
   116	  }
   117	}
````

````text
    21	    setCanvasLayerHidden(node, isLedgerTab);
    22	  });
    23	  if (!ledger) {
    24	    content.querySelectorAll('.ledger-node, .ledger-relationships').forEach((node) => node.remove());
    25	    telemetry('render-ledger-surface', { activeTab: state.activeTab, mode: 'static' });
    26	    return;
    27	  }
    28	  const marquee = content.querySelector('.marquee');
    29	  const activeZoneIds = new Set<string>();
    30	  const activeCardIds = new Set<string>();
    31	  const cards = Array.isArray(ledger.cards) ? ledger.cards as Array<Record<string, unknown>> : [];
    32	  const annotations = Array.isArray(ledger.annotations) ? ledger.annotations as Array<Record<string, unknown>> : [];
    33	  const relationships = Array.isArray(ledger.relationships) ? ledger.relationships as Array<Record<string, unknown>> : [];
    34	  const zoneAttribution = ensureZoneAttributionCache('render-ledger-surface');
    35	  for (const zone of annotations) {
    36	    const id = String(zone.id ?? '');
    37	    activeZoneIds.add(id);
    38	    const selector = zone.variant === 'group' ? `[data-group-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"].ledger-node` : `[data-zone-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"].ledger-node`;
    39	    const node = patchLedgerZone(zone, content.querySelector(selector) as HTMLElement | null);
    40	    if (!node.parentElement) content.insertBefore(node, marquee);
    41	  }
    42	  for (const card of cards) {
    43	    const id = String(card.id ?? '');

    47	  }
````

````text
     2	 * WHAT: Commits active-ledger geometry for one explicit selection.

     5	import { state, type SelectionState } from '../../state.js';

     7	import { selectedLedgerGeometryPayload } from '../helper/active-ledger-geometry.js';
     8	
     9	export async function commitSelectedLedgerGeometry(selection: Partial<SelectionState> = state.selection): Promise<boolean> {
    10	  // WHAT: Skip static canvases that have no active ledger mutation endpoint.
    11	  // WHY: Their geometry is persisted through the local runtime state path.
    12	  if (!state.activeLedger) return false;
    13	  const geometry = selectedLedgerGeometryPayload(selection);
    14	  const hasGeometry = Object.values(geometry).some((records) => Object.keys(records).length > 0);
    15	  // WHAT: Avoid an empty mutation when every selected id disappeared from the ledger.
    16	  // WHY: A no-op request would trigger unnecessary refresh and rendering work.
    17	  if (!hasGeometry) return false;
    18	  return commitActiveLedgerMutation({ action: 'patch-geometry', geometry }, { render: true });
    19	}
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Reads, normalizes, and patches active-ledger canvas geometry.
     3	 * WHY: Canvas effects need one geometry contract across cards, zones, and groups.

     5	import { state, type SelectionState } from '../../state.js';

     7	
     8	export type CanvasRect = { left: number; top: number; right: number; bottom: number; width: number; height: number };
     9	export type LedgerGeometry = { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number };
    10	
    11	type LedgerRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
    12	
    13	function finiteNumber(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
    14	  const number = Number(value);
    15	  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : fallback;
    16	}
    17	
    18	export function ledgerCardGeometry(card: LedgerRecord): LedgerGeometry {
    19	  return {
    20	    x: finiteNumber(card.x, 0),
    21	    y: finiteNumber(card.y, 0),
    22	    width: Math.max(220, finiteNumber(card.w ?? card.width, 280)),
    23	    height: Math.max(132, finiteNumber(card.h ?? card.height, 132))
    24	  };
    25	}
    26	
    27	export function ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation: LedgerRecord): LedgerGeometry {
    28	  const minWidth = annotation.variant === 'group' ? 220 : 180;
    29	  const minHeight = annotation.variant === 'group' ? 160 : 140;
    30	  return {
    31	    x: finiteNumber(annotation.x, 0),
    32	    y: finiteNumber(annotation.y, 0),
    33	    width: Math.max(minWidth, finiteNumber(annotation.width ?? annotation.w, 280)),
    34	    height: Math.max(minHeight, finiteNumber(annotation.height ?? annotation.h, 180))
    35	  };
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function geometryToRect(geometry: LedgerGeometry): CanvasRect {
    39	  return {
    40	    left: geometry.x,
    41	    top: geometry.y,
    42	    right: geometry.x + geometry.width,
    43	    bottom: geometry.y + geometry.height,
    44	    width: geometry.width,
    45	    height: geometry.height
    46	  };
    47	}
    48	
    49	export function activeLedgerCardRectMap(): Map<string, CanvasRect> {
    50	  const ledger = state.activeLedger as { cards?: unknown } | null;
    51	  const cards = Array.isArray(ledger?.cards) ? ledger.cards as LedgerRecord[] : [];
    52	  const entries: Array<[string, CanvasRect]> = [];
    53	  for (const card of cards) {
    54	    const id = String(card.id ?? '');
    55	    if (id) entries.push([id, geometryToRect(ledgerCardGeometry(card))]);
    56	  }
    57	  return new Map(entries);
    58	}
    59	
    60	export function activeLedgerCardMap(): Map<string, LedgerRecord> {
    61	  const ledger = state.activeLedger as { cards?: unknown } | null;
    62	  const cards = Array.isArray(ledger?.cards) ? ledger.cards as LedgerRecord[] : [];
    63	  const entries: Array<[string, LedgerRecord]> = [];
    64	  for (const card of cards) {
    65	    const id = String(card.id ?? '');
    66	    if (id) entries.push([id, card]);
    67	  }
    68	  return new Map(entries);
    69	}
    70	
    71	export function activeLedgerAnnotationMap(): Map<string, LedgerRecord> {
    72	  const ledger = state.activeLedger as { annotations?: unknown } | null;
    73	  const annotations = Array.isArray(ledger?.annotations) ? ledger.annotations as LedgerRecord[] : [];
    74	  const entries: Array<[string, LedgerRecord]> = [];
    75	  for (const annotation of annotations) {
    76	    const id = String(annotation.id ?? '');
    77	    if (id) entries.push([id, annotation]);
    78	  }
    79	  return new Map(entries);
    80	}
    81	
    82	export function patchLedgerCardGeometry(card: LedgerRecord, geometry: LedgerGeometry): LedgerGeometry {
    83	  const current = ledgerCardGeometry(card);
    84	  const next = {
    85	    x: Number.isFinite(geometry.x) ? geometry.x : current.x,
    86	    y: Number.isFinite(geometry.y) ? geometry.y : current.y,
    87	    width: Number.isFinite(geometry.width) ? Math.max(220, geometry.width) : current.width,
    88	    height: Number.isFinite(geometry.height) ? Math.max(132, geometry.height) : current.height
    89	  };
    90	  card.x = next.x;
    91	  card.y = next.y;
    92	  card.w = next.width;
    93	  card.h = next.height;
    94	  return next;
    95	}
    96	
    97	export function patchLedgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation: LedgerRecord, geometry: LedgerGeometry): LedgerGeometry {
    98	  const current = ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
    99	  const minWidth = annotation.variant === 'group' ? 220 : 180;
   100	  const minHeight = annotation.variant === 'group' ? 160 : 140;
   101	  const next = {
   102	    x: Number.isFinite(geometry.x) ? geometry.x : current.x,
   103	    y: Number.isFinite(geometry.y) ? geometry.y : current.y,
   104	    width: Number.isFinite(geometry.width) ? Math.max(minWidth, geometry.width) : current.width,
   105	    height: Number.isFinite(geometry.height) ? Math.max(minHeight, geometry.height) : current.height
   106	  };
   107	  annotation.x = next.x;
   108	  annotation.y = next.y;
   109	  annotation.width = next.width;
   110	  annotation.height = next.height;
   111	  return ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
   112	}
   113	
   114	export function selectedLedgerGeometryPayload(selection: Partial<SelectionState> = state.selection): {
   115	  cards: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   116	  zones: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   117	  groups: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   118	} {
   119	  const current = cloneSelectionState(selection);
   120	  const cards = activeLedgerCardMap();
   121	  const annotations = activeLedgerAnnotationMap();
   122	  const payload = { cards: {}, zones: {}, groups: {} } as {
   123	    cards: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   124	    zones: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   125	    groups: Record<string, LedgerGeometry>;
   126	  };
   127	  for (const id of current.cardIds) {
   128	    const card = cards.get(id);
   129	    if (card) payload.cards[id] = ledgerCardGeometry(card);
   130	  }
   131	  for (const id of current.zoneIds) {
   132	    const annotation = annotations.get(id);
   133	    if (annotation) payload.zones[id] = ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
   134	  }
   135	  for (const id of current.groupIds) {
   136	    const annotation = annotations.get(id);
   137	    if (annotation) payload.groups[id] = ledgerAnnotationGeometry(annotation);
   138	  }
   139	  return payload;
   140	}
````

````text
     5	import { applyPersistedGeometry } from './apply-persisted-geometry.js';

     7	export function hydratePersistedGeometry(geometry: unknown): void {
     8	  const records = geometry && typeof geometry === 'object' ? geometry as Record<string, Record<string, Record<string, number>>> : {};
     9	  applyPersistedGeometry('[data-card-id]', 'cardId', records.cards, { minWidth: 250, minHeight: 132 });

     5	export function persistState(): void {
     6	  state.viewports = { ...(state.viewports ?? {}), [state.activeTab]: { ...state.viewport } };
     7	  if (state.activeTab === 'surface') state.surfaceViewport = { ...state.viewport };
     8	  localStorage.setItem('decision-os.canvas.state', JSON.stringify({ viewport: state.viewport, viewports: state.viewports, selection: state.selection, activeTab: state.activeTab, railCollapsed: state.railCollapsed, geometry: snapshotCanvasGeometry(), regionEdits: snapshotCanvasRegionEdits() }));
     9	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Watches card Markdown content files and reports changes to the HTTP event stream.
     3	 * WHY: direct file patches must refresh browser card content without requiring a manual reload.
     4	 */
     5	import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readdirSync, statSync, watch, type FSWatcher } from 'node:fs';
     6	import { extname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';
     7	
     8	export type CardContentChange = {
     9	  contentFile: string;
    10	  file: string;
    11	  kind: 'card-content' | 'thread-content';
    12	};
    13	
    14	export function watchCardContentFiles(input: { decisionOsRoot: string; onChange: (event: CardContentChange) => void }): { close(): void; watchedDirectories: number } {
    15	  const roots = [
    16	    { directory: resolve(input.decisionOsRoot, 'cards'), kind: 'card-content' as const },
    17	    { directory: resolve(input.decisionOsRoot, 'threads'), kind: 'thread-content' as const },
    18	  ];
    19	  const watchers = new Map<string, FSWatcher>();
    20	  const pendingEvents = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>();
    21	
    22	  function emitFile(file: string, kind: CardContentChange['kind']): void {
    23	    if (extname(file) !== '.md') return;
    24	    const existingTimer = pendingEvents.get(file);
    25	    if (existingTimer) clearTimeout(existingTimer);
    26	    pendingEvents.set(file, setTimeout(() => {
    27	      pendingEvents.delete(file);
    28	      const contentFile = `.decision-os/${relative(input.decisionOsRoot, file)}`;
    29	      input.onChange({ contentFile, file, kind });
    30	    }, 50));
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  function watchDirectory(directory: string, kind: CardContentChange['kind']): void {
    34	    if (!existsSync(directory) || watchers.has(directory)) return;
    35	    for (const entry of readdirSync(directory, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    36	      if (entry.isDirectory()) watchDirectory(join(directory, entry.name), kind);
    37	    }
    38	    const watcher = watch(directory, { persistent: false }, (_eventType, filename) => {
    39	      if (!filename) return;
    40	      const changed = resolve(directory, String(filename));
    41	      if (existsSync(changed) && statSync(changed).isDirectory()) {
    42	        watchDirectory(changed, kind);
    43	        return;
    44	      }
    45	      emitFile(changed, kind);
    46	    });
    47	    watchers.set(directory, watcher);
    48	  }
    49	
    50	  for (const root of roots) {
    51	    mkdirSync(root.directory, { recursive: true });
    52	    watchDirectory(root.directory, root.kind);
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  return {
    56	    close() {
    57	      for (const timer of pendingEvents.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
    58	      pendingEvents.clear();
    59	      for (const watcher of watchers.values()) watcher.close();
    60	      watchers.clear();
    61	    },
    62	    get watchedDirectories() {
    63	      return watchers.size;
    64	    },
    65	  };
    66	}

   143	      ? resolve(process.cwd(), 'frontend')
   144	      : resolve(process.cwd(), '..', 'frontend');

   146	  runtime.decisionOsRoot = decisionOsRoot;
   147	  if (payload.mode === 'dry-run') {
   148	    return { ok: true, port, server: { listening: false, port } };
   149	  }

   151	  const publishCardContentChange = (event: CardContentChange): void => {
   152	    const message = `event: card-content-change\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(event)}\n\n`;
   153	    for (const client of contentEventClients) client.write(message);
   154	  };
   155	  const publishLedgerContentChange = (event: AnyRecord): void => {
   156	    const message = `event: ledger-content-change\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(event)}\n\n`;
   157	    for (const client of contentEventClients) client.write(message);
   158	  };
   159	  runtime.onCodexRunSettled = (event: AnyRecord): void => {
   160	    void continueQueuedVoiceCodexAfterRun({
   161	      runtime,
   162	      ledgerId: String(event.ledgerId ?? ''),
   163	      cardId: String(event.cardId ?? event.outputCardId ?? ''),
   164	      threadId: String(event.threadId ?? ''),
   165	      runId: String(event.runId ?? ''),
   166	      onCardContentChange: publishCardContentChange,

   168	    });

   173	    writeFileSync(ledgerPath, JSON.stringify(ledger, null, 2));
   174	    response.end(JSON.stringify(loadLedgerContentFiles(ledger)));
   175	  };

   177	  const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {

   179	    if (tryServeDecisionOsAsset({ url, decisionOsRoot, response })) return;
   180	    if (url === '/api/debug/codex-continue' && request.method === 'POST') {
   181	      const bodyBuffer = await readRequestBuffer(request);
   182	      const debugPayload = (() => {
   183	        try {
   184	          return JSON.parse(bodyBuffer.toString('utf8') || '{}') as AnyRecord;
   185	        } catch {
   186	          return { parseError: true, rawLength: bodyBuffer.length };
   187	        }
   188	      })();
   189	      console.log(JSON.stringify({ codexContinueDebug: true, source: 'frontend', receivedAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...debugPayload }));
   190	      response.setHeader('content-type', 'application/json');
   191	      response.statusCode = 204;
   192	      response.end();
   193	      return;
   194	    }
   195	    if (url === '/api/ledger-content-events' && request.method === 'GET') {
   196	      response.writeHead(200, {
   197	        'cache-control': 'no-store',
   198	        connection: 'keep-alive',
   199	        'content-type': 'text/event-stream',
   200	      });
   201	      response.write(': connected\n\n');
   202	      contentEventClients.add(response);
   203	      request.on('close', () => contentEventClients.delete(response));
   204	      return;
   205	    }
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Patches one ledger-authored card into the canvas DOM.

     5	import { state } from '../../state.js';

     7	import { cardFields } from '../helper/card-fields.js';
     8	import { cardLabels } from '../helper/card-labels.js';

    10	import { applyZoneAttributionToCardElement, normalizeZoneAttribution, type ZoneAttribution } from '../helper/zone-attribution-cache.js';

    12	import { renderGeometry } from '../../canvas/helper/render-density.js';

    14	function directChildByClass(element: HTMLElement, className: string): HTMLElement | null {
    15	  for (const child of Array.from(element.children) as HTMLElement[]) {
    16	    if (child.className.split(/\s+/).includes(className)) return child;
    17	  }
    18	  return null;
    19	}
````
