````text
    21	export function patchLedgerCard(card: Record<string, unknown>, existing?: HTMLElement | null, attribution?: ZoneAttribution | Record<string, unknown> | null): HTMLElement {
    22	  const element = existing ?? document.createElement('article');
    23	  const id = String(card.id ?? '');
    24	  const labels = cardLabels(card);
    25	  const fields = cardFields(card);
    26	  const hasFieldTabs = fields.length > 0;
    27	  const activeTab = hasFieldTabs && state.cardUi?.activeTabByCardId?.[id] === 'fields' ? 'fields' : 'description';
    28	  const persistedStatus = cardPersistedWorkStatus(card);
    29	  const visibleStatus = resolveCardWorkStatus(card);
    30	  const detailVisible = element.className.split(/\s+/).includes('detail-visible');
    31	  const targetLedgerId = String(card.targetLedgerId ?? '').trim();
    32	  const cardType = String(card.cardType ?? '').trim();
    33	  element.className = `card ledger-node${detailVisible ? ' detail-visible' : ''}`;
    34	  element.dataset.cardId = id;
    35	  if (cardType) element.dataset.cardType = cardType;
    36	  else delete element.dataset.cardType;
    37	  if (targetLedgerId) element.dataset.targetLedgerId = targetLedgerId;
    38	  else delete element.dataset.targetLedgerId;
    39	  element.dataset.activeCardTab = activeTab;
    40	  element.dataset.threadId = `thread-${id}`;
    41	  element.dataset.ledgerNode = 'card';
    42	  element.dataset.cardStatus = persistedStatus;
    43	  element.dataset.cardWorkStatus = visibleStatus;
    44	  delete element.dataset.agentLastAnswer;
    45	  if (labels.length > 0) element.dataset.cardLabels = labels.join(',');
    46	  else delete element.dataset.cardLabels;
    47	  applyZoneAttributionToCardElement(element, normalizeZoneAttribution(attribution));
    48	  const width = Math.max(220, Number(card.w ?? 280));
    49	  const cardHeight = Number(card.h ?? card.height);
    50	  const fixedHeight = Math.max(132, Number.isFinite(cardHeight) ? cardHeight : 132);
    51	  const geometry = { x: Number(card.x ?? 0), y: Number(card.y ?? 0), width, height: fixedHeight };
    52	  const renderedGeometry = renderGeometry(geometry);
    53	  element.style.left = `${renderedGeometry.x}px`;
    54	  element.style.top = `${renderedGeometry.y}px`;
    55	  element.style.width = `${renderedGeometry.width}px`;
    56	  element.style.height = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    57	  element.style.minHeight = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    58	  element.dataset.sizeCacheWidth = String(width);
    59	  element.dataset.sizeCacheHeight = String(fixedHeight);
    60	  element.style.setProperty('--card-size-cache-width', `${width}px`);
    61	  element.style.setProperty('--card-size-cache-height', `${fixedHeight}px`);
    62	  const handles = createCardResizeHandles();
    63	  const mountedDetail = directChildByClass(element, 'ledger-card-detail-layer');
    64	  const detailLayer = mountedDetail ? renderLedgerCardDetailLayer(card, mountedDetail) : null;
    65	  const overview = renderLedgerCardOverviewLayer(card, id, visibleStatus);
    66	  element.replaceChildren(...handles, ...(detailLayer ? [detailLayer] : []), overview);
    67	  return element;
    68	}
     1	import { renderGeometry } from '../../canvas/helper/render-density.js';
     2	
     3	export function patchLedgerZone(zone: Record<string, unknown>, existing?: HTMLElement | null): HTMLElement {
     4	  const element = existing ?? document.createElement('article');
     5	  const id = String(zone.id ?? '');
     6	  const isGroup = zone.variant === 'group';
     7	  const variant = isGroup ? 'group-zone' : 'regular-zone';
     8	  element.className = `zone ${variant} ledger-node`;
     9	  delete element.dataset.groupId;
    10	  delete element.dataset.zoneId;
    11	  if (isGroup) element.dataset.groupId = id;
    12	  else element.dataset.zoneId = id;
    13	  element.dataset.threadId = `thread-${id}`;
    14	  element.dataset.ledgerNode = 'zone';
    15	  const geometry = {
    16	    x: Number(zone.x ?? 0),
    17	    y: Number(zone.y ?? 0),
    18	    width: Math.max(180, Number(zone.width ?? 280)),
    19	    height: Math.max(120, Number(zone.height ?? 180))
    20	  };
    21	  const renderedGeometry = renderGeometry(geometry);
    22	  element.style.left = `${renderedGeometry.x}px`;
    23	  element.style.top = `${renderedGeometry.y}px`;
    24	  element.style.width = `${renderedGeometry.width}px`;
    25	  element.style.height = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    26	  element.style.minHeight = `${renderedGeometry.height}px`;
    27	  if (typeof zone.color === 'string') element.style.setProperty('--zone-color', zone.color);
    28	  else element.style.removeProperty('--zone-color');
    29	  const handles = ['nw', 'ne', 'sw', 'se'].map((position) => {
    30	    const handle = document.createElement('div');
    31	    handle.className = `resize-handle ${position}`;
    32	    return handle;
    33	  });
    34	  const title = document.createElement('div');
    35	  title.className = 'zone-title';
    36	  title.textContent = String(zone.label ?? id);
    37	  element.replaceChildren(...handles, title);
    38	  return element;
    39	}
````

````text
   209	      detach(element);
   210	      markConnected(element, false);
   211	    },
   212	    setAttribute(name: string, value: string) {
   213	      attributes.set(name, value);
   214	      if (name === 'id') element.id = value;
   215	      if (name === 'class') element.className = value;
   216	      if (name.startsWith('data-')) element.dataset[dataKey(name.slice(5))] = value;
   217	    },
   218	    getAttribute(name: string) {
   219	      if (name === 'id') return element.id || null;
   220	      if (name === 'class') return element.className || null;
   221	      if (name.startsWith('data-')) return element.dataset[dataKey(name.slice(5))] ?? null;
   222	      return attributes.get(name) ?? null;
   223	    },
   224	    hasAttribute(name: string) {
   225	      if (name === 'id') return Boolean(element.id);
   226	      if (name === 'class') return Boolean(element.className);
   227	      if (name.startsWith('data-')) return element.dataset[dataKey(name.slice(5))] !== undefined;
   228	      return attributes.has(name);
   229	    },
   230	    removeAttribute(name: string) {
   231	      attributes.delete(name);
   232	      if (name === 'id') element.id = '';
   233	      if (name === 'class') element.className = '';
   234	      if (name.startsWith('data-')) delete element.dataset[dataKey(name.slice(5))];
   235	    },
   236	    addEventListener() {},
   237	    removeEventListener() {},
   238	    blur() {},
   239	    scrollTo(options?: unknown) {
   240	      const scroll = options as { left?: number; top?: number } | undefined;
   241	      if (Number.isFinite(scroll?.left)) element.scrollLeft = Number(scroll?.left);
   242	      if (Number.isFinite(scroll?.top)) element.scrollTop = Number(scroll?.top);
   243	    },
   244	    getBoundingClientRect() {
   245	      return { left: element.offsetLeft, top: element.offsetTop, width: element.offsetWidth, height: element.offsetHeight };
   246	    }
   247	  };
   248	  return element;
   249	}
````

````text
    17	type ThreadFakeElement = {

    44	function threadElement(className = ''): ThreadFakeElement {
    45	  const properties = new Map<string, string>();
    46	  const element: ThreadFakeElement = {
    47	    dataset: {},
    48	    className,
    49	    hidden: false,
    50	    textContent: '',
    51	    value: '',
    52	    children: [],
    53	    parentElement: null,
    54	    style: {
    55	      setProperty(name: string, value: string) {
    56	        properties.set(name, value);
    57	      },
    58	      getPropertyValue(name: string) {
    59	        return properties.get(name) ?? '';
    60	      }
    61	    },
    62	    classList: {
    63	      toggle(name: string, force?: boolean) {
    64	        const classes = new Set(element.className.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
    65	        const shouldAdd = force ?? !classes.has(name);
    66	        if (shouldAdd) classes.add(name);
    67	        else classes.delete(name);
    68	        element.className = [...classes].join(' ');
    69	      },
    70	      add(...names: string[]) {
    71	        const classes = new Set(element.className.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
    72	        for (const name of names) classes.add(name);
    73	        element.className = [...classes].join(' ');
    74	      },
    75	      remove(...names: string[]) {
    76	        const classes = new Set(element.className.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean));
    77	        for (const name of names) classes.delete(name);
    78	        element.className = [...classes].join(' ');
    79	      },
    80	      contains(name: string) {
    81	        return element.className.split(/\s+/).includes(name);
    82	      }
    83	    },
    84	    append(...nodes: ThreadFakeElement[]) {
    85	      for (const node of nodes) {
    86	        node.parentElement = element;
    87	        element.children.push(node);
    88	      }
    89	    },
    90	    replaceChildren(...nodes: ThreadFakeElement[]) {
    91	      for (const child of element.children) child.parentElement = null;
    92	      element.children = [];
    93	      element.append(...nodes);
    94	    },
    95	    querySelector(selector: string) {
    96	      if (selector === '.thread-actions') return element.children.find((child) => child.className.split(/\s+/).includes('thread-actions')) ?? null;
    97	      return null;
    98	    },
    99	    querySelectorAll() {
   100	      return [];
   101	    },

   104	    },
   105	    addEventListener() {}
   106	  };
   107	  return element;
   108	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Browser interaction proof for spec 9d1b7c36: The refresh system preserves canvas continuity during operator work.
     3	 * WHY: Refresh during an active drag must not change the card id committed on release.
     4	 */
     5	
     6	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     7	import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from 'node:child_process';
     8	import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     9	import { createServer } from 'node:net';
    10	import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
    11	import test from 'node:test';
    12	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
    13	import { chromium, type Browser, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
    14	
    15	type LedgerCard = {
    16	  id?: string;
    17	  x?: number;
    18	  y?: number;
    19	  w?: number;
    20	  h?: number;
    21	  width?: number;
    22	  height?: number;
    23	};
    24	
    25	type LedgerDocument = {
    26	  cards?: LedgerCard[];
    27	  annotations?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
    28	};
    29	
    30	type LedgerGeometryPatch = {
    31	  cards?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    32	  zones?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    33	  groups?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }>;
    34	};
    35	
    36	type PatchGeometryMutation = {
    37	  action?: string;
    38	  geometry?: LedgerGeometryPatch;
    39	};
    40	
    41	const repoRoot = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../..');
    42	const targetCardId = '9d1b7c36';
    43	const chromiumExecutablePath = '/snap/bin/chromium';
    44	
    45	test('The refresh system preserves canvas continuity during operator work.', async () => {
    46	  const originalLedger = readSpecsLedger();
    47	  const targetCard = originalLedger.cards?.find((card) => String(card.id ?? '') === targetCardId);
    48	  assert.ok(targetCard, `Expected specs ledger to contain card ${targetCardId}`);
    49	
    50	  const viewport = viewportForCard(targetCard);
    51	  const server = await startDecisionOsServer();
    52	  let browser: Browser | undefined;
    53	
    54	  try {
    55	    browser = await launchBrowser();
    56	    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 } });
    57	    const committedMutations: PatchGeometryMutation[] = [];
    58	
    59	    await seedCanvasViewport(page, viewport);
    60	    await page.route(`${server.url}/decision-os/specs`, async (route) => {
    61	      const request = route.request();
    62	      if (request.method() !== 'PATCH') {
    63	        await route.continue();
    64	        return;
    65	      }
    66	      const mutation = request.postDataJSON() as PatchGeometryMutation;
    67	      if (mutation.action === 'patch-geometry') committedMutations.push(mutation);
    68	      await route.fulfill({
    69	        status: 200,
    70	        contentType: 'application/json',
    71	        body: JSON.stringify(applyGeometryPatch(originalLedger, mutation.geometry ?? {}))
    72	      });
    73	    });
    74	
    75	    await page.goto(`${server.url}/specs`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
    76	    await page.waitForFunction((cardId) => {
    77	      const state = (window as Window & { __coreState?: { activeLedger?: { cards?: Array<{ id?: string }> } } }).__coreState;
    78	      return Boolean(state?.activeLedger?.cards?.some((card) => String(card.id ?? '') === cardId));
    79	    }, targetCardId);
    80	
    81	    const card = page.locator(`[data-card-id="${targetCardId}"].ledger-node`);
    82	    await card.waitFor({ state: 'visible' });
    83	    const cardBox = await card.boundingBox();
    84	    assert.ok(cardBox, `Expected card ${targetCardId} to have browser geometry`);
    85	
    86	    const start = { x: cardBox.x + cardBox.width / 2, y: cardBox.y + cardBox.height / 2 };
    87	    await page.mouse.move(start.x, start.y);
    88	    await page.mouse.down();
    89	    await page.mouse.move(start.x + 40, start.y + 10);
    90	
    91	    const refreshCountBefore = await refreshCompletionCount(page);
    92	    await page.locator('[data-action="refresh"]').evaluate((button) => {
    93	      button.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    94	    });
    95	    await page.waitForFunction((previousCount) => {
    96	      const telemetry = ((window as Window & { __coreTelemetry?: Array<{ name?: string; args?: { source?: string } }> }).__coreTelemetry ?? []);
    97	      return telemetry.filter((entry) => entry.name === 'merge-refresh-state' && entry.args?.source === 'refresh-button').length > previousCount;
    98	    }, refreshCountBefore);
    99	
   100	    await page.mouse.move(start.x + 110, start.y + 30);
   101	    await page.mouse.up();
   102	
   103	    await waitFor(() => committedMutations.length > 0, 'Timed out waiting for patch-geometry commit');
   104	    const finalMutation = committedMutations.at(-1);
   105	    assert.ok(finalMutation?.geometry, 'Expected release to commit geometry');
   106	    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(finalMutation.geometry.cards ?? {}), [targetCardId]);
   107	    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(finalMutation.geometry.zones ?? {}), []);
   108	    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(finalMutation.geometry.groups ?? {}), []);
   109	    assert.equal(finalMutation.geometry.cards?.[targetCardId]?.x, Number(targetCard.x ?? 0) + 110);
   110	    assert.equal(finalMutation.geometry.cards?.[targetCardId]?.y, Number(targetCard.y ?? 0) + 30);
   111	  } finally {
   112	    await browser?.close();
   113	    await stopDecisionOsServer(server.process);
   114	  }
   115	});
   116	
   117	async function seedCanvasViewport(page: Page, viewport: { x: number; y: number; scale: number }): Promise<void> {
   118	  await page.addInitScript((input) => {
   119	    localStorage.setItem('decision-os.canvas.state', JSON.stringify({
   120	      activeTab: 'specs',
   121	      railCollapsed: false,
   122	      selection: { cardIds: [], zoneIds: [], groupIds: [] },
   123	      viewport: input,
   124	      viewports: { specs: input }
   125	    }));
   126	  }, viewport);
   127	}
   128	
   129	async function refreshCompletionCount(page: Page): Promise<number> {
   130	  return page.evaluate(() => {
   131	    const telemetry = ((window as Window & { __coreTelemetry?: Array<{ name?: string; args?: { source?: string } }> }).__coreTelemetry ?? []);
   132	    return telemetry.filter((entry) => entry.name === 'merge-refresh-state' && entry.args?.source === 'refresh-button').length;
   133	  });
   134	}
   135	
   136	function readSpecsLedger(): LedgerDocument {
   137	  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(repoRoot, '.decision-os/specs.json'), 'utf8')) as LedgerDocument;
   138	}
   139	
   140	function viewportForCard(card: LedgerCard): { x: number; y: number; scale: number } {
   141	  return {
   142	    x: 420 - Number(card.x ?? 0),
   143	    y: 240 - Number(card.y ?? 0),
   144	    scale: 1
   145	  };
   146	}
   147	
   148	function applyGeometryPatch(ledger: LedgerDocument, geometry: LedgerGeometryPatch): LedgerDocument {
   149	  const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ledger)) as LedgerDocument;
   150	  const cardPatches = geometry.cards ?? {};
   151	  next.cards = (next.cards ?? []).map((card) => {
   152	    const patch = cardPatches[String(card.id ?? '')];
   153	    return patch ? { ...card, x: patch.x, y: patch.y, w: patch.width, h: patch.height } : card;
   154	  });
   155	  return next;
   156	}
   157	
   158	async function launchBrowser(): Promise<Browser> {
   159	  return chromium.launch({
   160	    headless: true,
   161	    executablePath: existsSync(chromiumExecutablePath) ? chromiumExecutablePath : undefined,
   162	    args: ['--no-sandbox', '--disable-dev-shm-usage']
   163	  });
   164	}
   165	
   166	async function startDecisionOsServer(): Promise<{ process: ChildProcess; url: string }> {
   167	  const port = await freePort();
   168	  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
   169	  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [resolve(repoRoot, 'bin/decision-os-server.mjs')], {
   170	    cwd: repoRoot,
   171	    detached: true,
   172	    env: { ...process.env, HOST: '127.0.0.1', PORT: String(port) },
   173	    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
   174	  });
   175	  const output: string[] = [];
   176	  child.stdout?.on('data', (chunk) => output.push(String(chunk)));
   177	  child.stderr?.on('data', (chunk) => output.push(String(chunk)));
   178	  await waitFor(async () => {
   179	    assert.equal(child.exitCode, null, `decision-os server exited early:\n${output.join('')}`);
   180	    const response = await fetch(`${url}/specs`, { method: 'HEAD' }).catch(() => undefined);
   181	    return Boolean(response?.ok);
   182	  }, `Timed out waiting for decision-os server at ${url}/specs`);
   183	  return { process: child, url };
   184	}
   185	
   186	async function stopDecisionOsServer(child: ChildProcess): Promise<void> {
   187	  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
   188	  if (child.pid) {
   189	    try {
   190	      process.kill(-child.pid, 'SIGTERM');
   191	    } catch {
   192	      child.kill('SIGTERM');
   193	    }
   194	  } else {
   195	    child.kill('SIGTERM');
   196	  }
   197	  const exited = await Promise.race([waitForExit(child).then(() => true), delay(2000).then(() => false)]);
   198	  if (!exited && child.pid) {
   199	    try {
   200	      process.kill(-child.pid, 'SIGKILL');
   201	    } catch {
   202	      child.kill('SIGKILL');
   203	    }
   204	  }
   205	}
   206	
   207	async function freePort(): Promise<number> {
   208	  const server = createServer();
   209	  await new Promise<void>((resolveListen) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolveListen));
   210	  const address = server.address();
   211	  assert.ok(address && typeof address === 'object', 'Expected an ephemeral TCP port');
   212	  const port = address.port;
   213	  await new Promise<void>((resolveClose) => server.close(() => resolveClose()));
   214	  return port;
   215	}
   216	
   217	async function waitFor(check: () => boolean | Promise<boolean>, message: string): Promise<void> {
   218	  const deadline = Date.now() + 10000;
   219	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
   220	    if (await check()) return;
   221	    await delay(50);
   222	  }
   223	  assert.fail(message);
   224	}
   225	
   226	function waitForExit(child: ChildProcess): Promise<void> {
   227	  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return Promise.resolve();
   228	  return new Promise((resolveExit) => child.once('exit', () => resolveExit()));
   229	}
   230	
   231	function delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
   232	  return new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, ms));
   233	}
````

````text
    11	export function selectTarget(kind: string, id: string, additive: boolean): void {
    12	  if (!id) return;
    13	  telemetry('resolve-selection-target', { kind, id, additive });
    14	  if (!additive) selectThread(threadIdForTarget(kind, id));
    15	  if (!additive) state.selection = { cardIds: [], zoneIds: [], groupIds: [] };
    16	  const key = kind === 'card' ? 'cardIds' : kind === 'zone' ? 'zoneIds' : 'groupIds';
    17	  if (additive && state.selection[key].includes(id)) {
    18	    state.selection[key] = state.selection[key].filter((selectedId: string) => selectedId !== id);
    19	  } else if (!state.selection[key].includes(id)) {
    20	    state.selection[key].push(id);
    21	  }
    22	  if (kind === 'zone') {
    23	    const intersectingCards = cardsIntersectingZone(id);
    24	    for (const cardId of intersectingCards) {
    25	      if (!state.selection.cardIds.includes(cardId)) state.selection.cardIds.push(cardId);
    26	    }
    27	    telemetry('resolve-zone-intersections', { zoneId: id, cardIds: intersectingCards });
    28	  }
    29	  if (kind === 'group') {
    30	    const membership = resolveGroupMembership(id);
    31	    state.selection.groupIds = membership.groupIds;
    32	    state.selection.zoneIds = membership.zoneIds;
    33	    state.selection.cardIds = membership.cardIds;
    34	    telemetry('resolve-group-membership', { groupId: id, selection: state.selection });
    35	  }
    36	  renderSelectionState();
    37	  if (state.threadPanelOpen || state.activeTool === 'thread') renderThreadPanel();
    38	  else renderTelemetry();
    39	}

     4	
     5	export function renderSelectionState(): void {
     6	  const connectedIds = connectedCardIds(state.selection.cardIds);
     7	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-card-id]').forEach((node) => {
     8	    const element = node as HTMLElement;
     9	    element.classList.toggle('selected', state.selection.cardIds.includes(element.dataset.cardId));
    10	    element.classList.toggle('connected', connectedIds.includes(element.dataset.cardId ?? ''));
    11	  });
    12	  document.querySelectorAll('[data-zone-id]').forEach((node) => {

    18	    element.classList.toggle('selected', state.selection.groupIds.includes(element.dataset.groupId));
    19	  });
    20	  renderCanvasControlOverlay();
    21	}
````

````text
---
name: analysis
description: Launch deep static codebase analysis with a native subagent (not agent mcp), correlate implementation against specs, identify gaps/omissions/drift, and produce a working document with repo intent, current iteration intent, issues, and remediation paths. Use when the user says "agentanalysis" or asks for a full code/spec drift analysis and operator-facing decision summary.
---

# Analysis

## A. Formatting Contract

1. **Headings:** use `H2` card sections with uppercase letters: `## A. Scope`, `## B. Contract`, `## C. Acceptance Criteria`.
2. **Dividers:** put `---` between card sections.
3. **Lists:** write section content as numbered list items: `1.`, `2.`, `3.`.
4. **Bold:** use **bold** for the important words that carry the point.
5. **Backticks:** use `backticks` for technical, secondary, exact, or literal terms: file paths, routes, config keys, commands, IDs, statuses, branch names, code symbols, and literal values.

Execute a repository-wide static analysis using a native subagent (not agent mcp).

## Workflow

1. Spawn a native subagent and assign it deep static analysis coverage over the codebase.
2. Read canonical specs/architecture docs and correlate them with current implementation.
3. Identify and classify:
- Gaps (missing required behavior)
- Omissions (partially implemented or unimplemented scope)
- Drift (implementation no longer matching specification)
4. Create a new working document in the repo that includes:
- Intent of the repository
- Intent of the current iteration
- Full issue list
- Remediation paths per issue
5. Produce a concise, human-readable operator summary focused on decisions and tradeoffs.

## Execution Requirements

- Use only native subagents for analysis; do not use agent-mcp wrappers for core analysis execution.
- Prefer deterministic evidence: file paths, symbols, and behavior deltas tied to specs.
- Keep findings actionable by pairing each issue with a concrete remediation path.
- Keep the final operator summary short and decision-oriented.

## Deliverable Template

Use this structure for the generated working document:

1. Repository Intent
2. Current Iteration Intent
3. Findings
4. Remediation Paths
5. Operator Decision Summary
````

````text
   101	function codexToolAction(command: string): string {

   105	  if (/(^|[\s;&|()])(?:cat|sed|nl|head|tail|less|wc)(?=\s|$)/i.test(command)) return 'Read';
   106	  if (/(^|[\s;&|()])(?:npm|pnpm|yarn|node|tsx|tsc|vitest|jest|playwright|pytest)(?=\s|$)/i.test(command)) return 'Ran';
   107	  return 'Ran';

   110	function shortenText(value: string, maxLength: number): string {
   111	  const compact = value.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
   112	  if (compact.length <= maxLength) return compact;
   113	  const headLength = Math.max(24, Math.floor(maxLength * 0.62));
   114	  const tailLength = Math.max(12, maxLength - headLength - 5);
   115	  return `${compact.slice(0, headLength).trimEnd()} ... ${compact.slice(-tailLength).trimStart()}`;
   116	}

   158	function threadImageSizes(value: unknown): ThreadImageSizes {

   160	  const sizes: ThreadImageSizes = {};

   173	function persistThreadImageSize(input: { threadId: string; note: Record<string, unknown>; source: string; width: number; height: number }): void {

   193	export function renderThreadNotes(): void {
   194	  const existing = document.querySelector('.thread-note-list') as HTMLElement | null;

   204	  }

   221	    const body = renderLedgerCardMarkdown(noteText(note), {

   248	    item.append(noteBody);
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Merges locally optimistic thread notes into an incoming server ledger.
     3	 * WHY: Server refreshes can be stale while note upload or transcription reconciliation is still in flight.
     4	 */
     5	import { state } from '../../state.js';
     6	import { normalizeLedgerNotes } from './normalize-ledger-notes.js';
     7	import { normalizeDeletedNoteIds } from './normalize-deleted-note-ids.js';
     8	
     9	function imageSizesRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
    10	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;
    11	  return Object.keys(value).length > 0 ? value as Record<string, unknown> : null;
    12	}
    13	
    14	export function mergeLocalThreadNotes(ledger: Record<string, any> | null): Record<string, any> | null {
    15	  if (!ledger || typeof ledger !== 'object') return ledger;
    16	  const localNotes = state.activeLedger ? normalizeLedgerNotes(state.activeLedger) : undefined;
    17	  const localDeleted = state.activeLedger ? normalizeDeletedNoteIds(state.activeLedger) : {};
    18	  if (!localNotes || typeof localNotes !== 'object') return ledger;
    19	  const nextNotes = { ...normalizeLedgerNotes(ledger) } as Record<string, Array<Record<string, any>>>;
    20	  const nextDeleted = { ...normalizeDeletedNoteIds(ledger) } as Record<string, string[]>;
    21	  for (const [threadId, deletedIds] of Object.entries(localDeleted)) {
    22	    const mergedDeleted = new Set([...(nextDeleted[threadId] ?? []), ...(Array.isArray(deletedIds) ? deletedIds : [])].map((id) => String(id)));
    23	    if (mergedDeleted.size > 0) nextDeleted[threadId] = Array.from(mergedDeleted);
    24	  }
    25	  for (const [threadId, deletedIds] of Object.entries(nextDeleted)) {
    26	    const deletedSet = new Set((Array.isArray(deletedIds) ? deletedIds : []).map((id) => String(id)));
    27	    if (!deletedSet.size || !Array.isArray(nextNotes[threadId])) continue;
    28	    nextNotes[threadId] = nextNotes[threadId].filter((note) => !deletedSet.has(String(note.id ?? '')));
    29	  }
    30	  for (const [threadId, notes] of Object.entries(localNotes as Record<string, Array<Record<string, any>>>)) {
    31	    if (!Array.isArray(notes)) continue;
    32	    const deletedSet = new Set((nextDeleted[threadId] ?? []).map((id) => String(id)));
    33	    const merged = Array.isArray(nextNotes[threadId]) ? [...nextNotes[threadId]] : [];
    34	    for (const localNote of notes) {
    35	      const noteId = String(localNote.id ?? '');
    36	      if (!noteId) continue;
    37	      if (deletedSet.has(noteId)) continue;
    38	      const localImageSizes = imageSizesRecord(localNote.imageSizes);
    39	      const existingIndex = merged.findIndex((note) => String(note.id ?? '') === noteId);
    40	      if (localImageSizes && existingIndex >= 0) {
    41	        const existingImageSizes = imageSizesRecord(merged[existingIndex].imageSizes) ?? {};
    42	        merged[existingIndex] = {
    43	          ...merged[existingIndex],
    44	          imageSizes: { ...existingImageSizes, ...localImageSizes }
    45	        };
    46	      }
    47	      if (!localNote?.optimistic) continue;
    48	      if (existingIndex >= 0) merged[existingIndex] = { ...merged[existingIndex], ...localNote };
    49	      else merged.push(localNote);
    50	    }
    51	    if (merged.length > 0 || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(nextNotes, threadId)) nextNotes[threadId] = merged;
    52	  }
    53	  ledger.notes = nextNotes;
    54	  ledger.deletedNoteIds = nextDeleted;
    55	  return ledger;
    56	}
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Normalizes runtime ledger notes into a thread-id keyed note map.
     3	 * WHY: Legacy ledgers may load with notes as [], but optimistic note state must persist by thread id.
     4	 */
     5	export function normalizeLedgerNotes(ledger: { notes?: unknown }): Record<string, Array<Record<string, any>>> {
     6	  if (!ledger.notes || Array.isArray(ledger.notes) || typeof ledger.notes !== 'object') {
     7	    ledger.notes = {};
     8	  }
     9	  return ledger.notes as Record<string, Array<Record<string, any>>>;
    10	}
````

````text
External card and thread content refreshes must preserve live interaction state when possible. If a content file Markdown edit arrives while voice recording is active, the browser must defer the ledger content refresh until recording stops, then flush the pending refresh before transcription upload continues.

The refresh must not clear the selected thread, interrupt capture, cancel the voice note, or change the active ledger tab.
The HTTP server must watch card Markdown content files under `.decision-os/cards/**/*.md` and thread Markdown content files under `.decision-os/threads/**/*.md` for the active workspace. A direct file edit must emit a server-sent event on `/api/ledger-content-events` without requiring a manual browser reload.

The browser must subscribe to this stream during boot and refresh the active ledger content when a card or thread content file changes, preserving the current route and viewport state.
````

````text
::html[Context report summary](.decision-os/cards/tasks-system/assets/context-report-summary/context-report-summary.html)

---

## A. Product Context

1. **Current product state.** `decision-os` is a local browser canvas, a TypeScript backend ledger server, and workspace-local `.decision-os` data. The active repo workspace is dogfooding the product through `.decision-os/state.json`, which currently exposes `specs`, `data`, `performances`, and `tasks-system`.
2. **Surface area.** The current surface includes route-addressed ledger tabs, a ledgers overview canvas, cards, zones, groups, relationships, canvas pan/zoom, selection, clipboard copy/paste, card resize, card Markdown, card fields, image carousels, HTML card assets, right-side threads, voice notes, transcription retry, live refresh, the `ledger-cli`, and the `generator-cli`.
3. **Active workflows.** Operators inspect and edit ledgers on the canvas, create or move cards/zones/groups, review specs and performance evidence, paste images/files into threads, record voice notes, ask agents to treat unanswered notes, maintain card and zone summaries, and use report/mockup cards for PO analysis work.
4. **Actors and roles.** The **operator** owns decisions, notes, approvals, and workspace intent. The **agent** reads threads, changes repo or ledger state, and replies in `# AGENT` sections. The **watcher** concept monitors unanswered notes. The **backend server** owns filesystem hydration and mutation. The **frontend runtime** owns transient interaction state. The **ledger CLI** is the maintenance and agent queue surface. The **OpenAI transcription provider** is optional and configured per workspace.
5. **Adoption state.** Adoption evidence is internal and active: the repo workspace contains `289` spec cards, `40` data-model cards, `9` performance cards, and `27` task-system cards. No external customer analytics, production usage metrics, or support evidence were found in this research pass.
6. **Active specs.** `specs.json` covers boot, navigation, canvas rendering, zones, selection, cards, threads, voice, refresh, groups, relationships, media, CLI tooling, generator tooling, and implementation architecture. `tasks-system.json` is the current Next Features surface and contains work around watcher loops, summary maintenance, task queues, report UX, and goal-object UX.
7. **Known friction.** Threads currently carry too many responsibilities: conversation history, work queue, lock, status model, progress log, and recovery surface. Performance notes show drag and pan risk around browser frame production, layout-position writes, zone label reads, overlay rerenders, rich card surfaces, and relationship routing. Report UX notes show unresolved product boundaries for whether reports should be a card view, ledger type, goal cockpit tab, or embedded reader.

---

## B. Technical Context

1. **Platform.** The backend is a Node/TypeScript HTTP server run through `tsx`; the frontend is a frameworkless TypeScript browser runtime served without a bundler; tests use Node's native test runner and Playwright-style browser specs; card syntax highlighting uses `highlight.js`.
2. **Architecture.** The repo is split into `backend`, `frontend`, `ledger-cli`, `generator-cli`, `shared`, `tests`, `operations`, and workspace `.decision-os` files. The launcher `bin/decision-os-server.mjs` derives `DECISION_OS_FRONTEND_ROOT` and `TSX_TSCONFIG_PATH`, then starts `backend/src/server.ts` from the target workspace cwd.
3. **Services and APIs.** The backend serves app routes `/`, `/ledgers`, and `/<ledgerId>`, state and ledger APIs under `/decision-os/state`, `/decision-os/<tab>`, `/decision-os/ledgers`, and `/decision-os/ledgers-canvas`, live refresh at `/api/ledger-content-events`, uploads at `/api/thread-image-upload`, `/api/thread-file-upload`, and `/api/voice-upload`, and transcription at `/api/transcribe` plus `/api/transcribe/retry`.
4. **Data model.** `.decision-os/state.json` owns the ledger registry. Each ledger JSON owns `cards`, `annotations`, `relationships`, `viewport`, `threadFiles`, and related structured fields. Card bodies are durable Markdown files referenced by `comment.contentFile`. Thread histories are durable Markdown files under `.decision-os/threads/...`. Image layout persists through `imageSizes` in card JSON.
5. **State ownership.** The filesystem is the canonical state store. The backend hydrates card Markdown and thread files into ledger responses, strips hydrated notes before writing, validates mutations, writes ledger JSON, writes card content files, and writes thread content files. The frontend owns transient state such as `activeTab`, `viewport`, `selection`, active tool, card tab state, open thread, draft text, scroll position, clipboard, and voice capture state.
6. **Integrations.** Workspace settings in `.decision-os/.settings.json` can configure transcription with `OPENAI_API_KEY` or `openaiApiKey` and `gpt-4o-mini-transcribe`. The server also integrates with local filesystem watchers, local static asset serving, browser media capture, and the local CLI tools.
7. **Permissions and security.** The default server host is `127.0.0.1`. Workspace asset serving is constrained to the active `.decision-os` root and allowed image, thread-file, or active-card asset paths. API keys and uploaded voice files are intended to stay local and uncommitted.
8. **Observability.** The frontend and generator surfaces emit local telemetry facts. Performance evidence is stored in `operations/canvas-performance-study-2026-05-29.md` and `.decision-os/cards/performances/...`. Verification surfaces include `npm run typecheck:frontend`, `npm run typecheck:backend`, `npm run test:browser`, `npm run test:front-back`, and live verification scripts under `tools/live-verify`.
9. **Deployment.** Current deployment is local workspace execution, not hosted SaaS. Operator-facing sessions should run the detached launcher from the target workspace cwd, redirect logs under `/tmp`, and verify the HTTP route before reporting a URL.

---

## C. UX Context

1. **Journeys.** A user opens a ledger route, reviews the canvas, selects or creates cards/zones/groups, edits geometry or content, opens the thread panel with `A`, writes or records notes, receives agent replies, reviews card media or report mockups, and can use CLI workflows for maintenance or agent treatment.
2. **Screens and regions.** The main UX includes the tab registry, full-canvas ledger surface, toolbox, canvas controls, card detail layer, card overview layer, card description and fields tabs, relationship overlays, zone/group labels, deletion confirmation modals, right-side thread inspector, terminal-style composer, voice dock, telemetry/status copy, and the hidden ledgers overview.
3. **States.** Important UI states include route mode, active ledger, viewport, zoom/detail mode, active tool, selection, grouped selection, clipboard, open card tab, low-detail versus hydrated card rendering, thread open/closed, draft restored, scroll restored, optimistic note pending, voice recording, transcription uploading, transcription failed, retry available, modal open, and external refresh received.
4. **Entry points.** Primary entry points are `/`, `/ledgers`, `/<ledgerId>`, the tab registry, card/zone/group controls, the toolbox, keyboard shortcuts, thread buttons, thread upload/paste controls, voice controls, `ledger-cli`, and server launch commands.
5. **Handoffs.** Operator notes hand off to agents through thread files. Agent replies hand back through `# AGENT` notes and durable card edits. Voice capture hands off from browser media capture to upload, transcription, optimistic note update, and retry. External filesystem edits hand off to the client through server-sent refresh events. Card Markdown hands off to backend hydration and frontend Markdown/media rendering.
6. **Information architecture.** The current workspace separates **Specs** for behavior contracts, **Data** for base classes and relationships, **Performances** for measured canvas performance evidence, and **Tasks System** for Next Features. At the object level, cards, zones, groups, relationships, and threads are the main navigable units.
7. **Accessibility.** The implemented keyboard contract includes `A`, `X`, `Esc`, `Del`, `Ctrl+C`, `Ctrl+V`, and `Ctrl+D`. Deletion confirmations support keyboard handling. Some rendered controls expose labels or titles. Full WCAG coverage, screen-reader behavior, focus order, and color-contrast audits were not verified in this pass.
8. **Copy and language.** Agent replies must be English. Thread copy uses `# OPERATOR` and `# AGENT` sections. Durable card content should stay concrete, implementation-ready, and traceable to source files or ledgers. The in-app Keys panel should remain the operator-facing keyboard contract.
9. **Interaction constraints.** Expensive card detail is hydrated only when detail is visible. Pan/zoom and drag must preserve responsive frame production. Delete actions require confirmation. Thread and voice tooling must not steal editing keystrokes. Media dimensions must persist independent of canvas zoom. HTML embeds must stay under allowed card asset paths.

---

## D. Context Boundaries

1. **In scope.** This context covers the current `decision-os` repo, the active repo-local `.decision-os` workspace, existing code, existing docs, active ledgers, task cards, performance notes, thread model, CLI model, and local runtime behavior visible from source inspection.
2. **Out of scope.** This context does not cover a hosted deployment, multi-tenant permissions, billing, external customer research, market positioning, production analytics, support tickets, or non-repo workspaces.
3. **Unverified areas.** This pass did not start a browser session, run automated tests, inspect live decision-os workspace state, validate a real transcription API key, measure current browser performance, audit accessibility with assistive technology, or confirm external operator adoption beyond repo evidence.
4. **Source gaps.** There is no discovered analytics baseline, customer interview set, support ticket sample, hosted deployment runbook, centralized telemetry sink, or current CI result attached to this note. Some README examples still describe older `tabs` wording while current state uses a `ledgers` registry.
5. **Cross-team dependencies.** The only confirmed dependency outside the local repo is optional OpenAI transcription configuration. Product-boundary decisions for reports, work queues, watcher agents, and goal objects remain operator-owned unless a future card assigns explicit ownership.
6. **Evidence sources.** Primary evidence came from `README.md`, `.decision-os/state.json`, `.decision-os/specs.json`, `.decision-os/data.json`, `.decision-os/performances.json`, `.decision-os/tasks-system.json`, `backend/src/business/server/helper/create-http-server.ts`, `backend/src/business/ledger/helper/apply-ledger-mutation.ts`, `frontend/src/runtime/boot/controller/boot-surface.ts`, `frontend/src/runtime/ledger/effect/render-ledger-surface.ts`, `frontend/src/runtime/input/controller/handle-keyboard.ts`, `frontend/src/runtime/thread/effect/render-thread-panel.ts`, `.decision-os/cards/tasks-system/card-agent-autonomy-work-queue.md`, `.decision-os/cards/tasks-system/card-2395850a-c528-4689-a138-73a30b684602.md`, and `.decision-os/cards/performances/...`.
````

````text
    21	export function createLedgerCardTitle(card: Record<string, unknown>, id: string, className = 'ledger-card-title'): HTMLElement {

    24	  appendTitleText(title, String(card.title ?? id));

    28	export function createLedgerCardTitleEditButton(card: Record<string, unknown>, id: string): HTMLButtonElement {

    43	  row.replaceChildren(createLedgerCardTitle(card, id), createLedgerCardTitleEditButton(card, id));
    44	  return row;

    23	function selectionMetric(label: string, key: string, options: readonly string[]): HTMLElement {
    24	  const item = document.createElement('label');
    25	  item.className = 'codex-run-metric codex-run-metric--control';

    28	  name.textContent = label;
    29	  const select = document.createElement('select');
    30	  select.className = 'codex-run-select';
    31	  select.dataset[key] = '';

    34	  for (const value of options) {
    35	    const option = document.createElement('option');
    36	    option.value = value;
    37	    option.textContent = value;
    38	    select.append(option);

    40	  select.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => event.stopPropagation());
    41	  select.addEventListener('click', (event) => event.stopPropagation());
    42	  item.replaceChildren(name, select);
    43	  return item;
    44	}
    45	
    46	export function renderCardSkillRunWidget(card: Record<string, unknown>): HTMLElement | null {
    47	  const cardId = String(card.id ?? '');
    48	  const ledgerId = String(state.activeTab ?? '').trim();
    49	  const runId = cardCodexRunId(card);
    50	  if (!cardId || !ledgerId || !runId) return null;
    51	
    52	  const widget = document.createElement('section');
    53	  widget.className = 'codex-run-widget';
    54	  widget.dataset.runId = runId;
    55	  widget.dataset.runStatus = 'running';
    56	
    57	  const body = document.createElement('div');
    58	  body.className = 'codex-run-body';
    59	
    60	  const header = document.createElement('div');
    61	  header.className = 'codex-run-header';
    62	  const status = document.createElement('span');
    63	  status.className = 'codex-run-status';
    64	  status.dataset.codexRunStatus = '';
    65	  status.textContent = 'RUNNING';
    66	  const cancel = document.createElement('button');
    67	  cancel.type = 'button';
    68	  cancel.className = 'codex-run-cancel terminal-button terminal-button--stop terminal-button--compact';
    69	  cancel.dataset.codexRunCancel = '';
    70	  cancel.title = 'Cancel Codex run';
    71	  cancel.setAttribute('aria-label', cancel.title);
    72	  cancel.textContent = 'Cancel';
    73	  const resume = document.createElement('button');
    74	  resume.type = 'button';
    75	  resume.className = 'codex-run-continue terminal-button terminal-button--compact';
    76	  resume.dataset.codexRunContinue = '';
    77	  resume.hidden = true;
    78	  resume.title = 'Continue Codex session';
    79	  resume.setAttribute('aria-label', resume.title);
    80	  resume.textContent = 'Continue';
    81	  const newSession = document.createElement('button');
    82	  newSession.type = 'button';
    83	  newSession.className = 'codex-run-new-session terminal-button terminal-button--compact';
    84	  newSession.dataset.codexRunNewSession = '';
    85	  newSession.hidden = true;
    86	  newSession.title = 'Start a new Codex session';
    87	  newSession.setAttribute('aria-label', newSession.title);
    88	  newSession.textContent = 'New session';
    89	  const actions = document.createElement('div');
    90	  actions.className = 'codex-run-actions';
    91	  actions.replaceChildren(cancel, resume, newSession);
    92	  header.replaceChildren(status, actions);
    93	
    94	  const timer = document.createElement('time');
    95	  timer.className = 'codex-run-timer';
    96	  timer.dataset.codexRunTimer = '';
    97	  timer.textContent = '0:00';
    98	
    99	  const metrics = document.createElement('div');
   100	  metrics.className = 'codex-run-metrics';
   101	  metrics.replaceChildren(
   102	    metric('Tools', '0', 'codexRunTools'),
   103	    metric('Text', '0', 'codexRunMessages'),
   104	    metric('Files', '0', 'codexRunFiles')
   105	  );
   106	
   107	  const metadata = document.createElement('div');
   108	  metadata.className = 'codex-run-metadata';
   109	  metadata.dataset.codexRunMetadata = '';
   110	  metadata.hidden = true;
   111	  metadata.replaceChildren(
   112	    metric('Source', '', 'codexRunSource'),
   113	    selectionMetric('Model', 'codexRunModel', codexModelOptions),
   114	    selectionMetric('Effort', 'codexRunEffort', codexEffortOptions)
   115	  );
   116	
   117	  const latest = document.createElement('p');
   118	  latest.className = 'codex-run-latest';
   119	  latest.dataset.codexRunLatest = '';
   120	  latest.textContent = 'Waiting for output';
   121	
   122	  body.replaceChildren(header, metadata, metrics, latest);
   123	  widget.replaceChildren(body, timer);
   124	  bindCardSkillRunWidget({ ledgerId, cardId, runId, element: widget });
   125	  return widget;
   126	}
````

````text
     1	/**
     2	 * WHAT: Renders the shared markdown body used by cards and thread notes.
     3	 * WHY: Markdown display must stay canonical across the canvas and the conversation ledger.
     4	 */
     5	import { appendInlineNodes } from './append-inline-nodes.js';
     6	import { parseLedgerCardMarkdown } from '../helper/parse-ledger-card-markdown.js';
     7	import { renderLedgerCardCodeBlock } from './render-ledger-card-code-block.js';
     8	import { renderLedgerCardHtmlEmbeds } from './render-ledger-card-html-embeds.js';
     9	import { renderLedgerCardMedia, type LedgerCardImageSizes } from './render-ledger-card-media.js';
    10	import { renderLedgerCardTable } from './render-ledger-card-table.js';
    11	
    12	type LedgerCardMarkdownOptions = {
    13	  cardId?: string;
    14	  imageSizes?: LedgerCardImageSizes;
    15	  mediaSurface?: 'card' | 'thread';
    16	  onImageResize?: (source: string, dimensions: { width: number; height: number }) => void;
    17	};
    18	
    19	export function renderLedgerCardMarkdown(markdown: string, options: LedgerCardMarkdownOptions = {}): HTMLElement {
    20	  const body = document.createElement('div');
    21	  body.className = 'ledger-card-body';
    22	
    23	  for (const block of parseLedgerCardMarkdown(markdown)) {
    24	    if (block.kind === 'heading') {
    25	      const heading = document.createElement(`h${Math.min(6, Math.max(1, block.level))}`);
    26	      heading.className = `ledger-card-heading ledger-card-heading-${block.level}`;
    27	      appendInlineNodes(heading, block.children, options);
    28	      body.appendChild(heading);
    29	      continue;
    30	    }
    31	    if (block.kind === 'list') {
    32	      const list = document.createElement('ul');
    33	      for (const item of block.items) {
    34	        const li = document.createElement('li');
    35	        appendInlineNodes(li, item, options);
    36	        list.appendChild(li);
    37	      }
    38	      body.appendChild(list);
    39	      continue;
    40	    }
    41	    if (block.kind === 'table') {
    42	      body.appendChild(renderLedgerCardTable(block, options));
    43	      continue;
    44	    }
    45	    if (block.kind === 'images') {
    46	      body.appendChild(renderLedgerCardMedia(block, options));
    47	      continue;
    48	    }
    49	    if (block.kind === 'htmlEmbeds') {
    50	      body.appendChild(renderLedgerCardHtmlEmbeds(block, options));
    51	      continue;
    52	    }
    53	    if (block.kind === 'code') {
    54	      body.appendChild(renderLedgerCardCodeBlock(block));
    55	      continue;
    56	    }
    57	    if (block.kind === 'hr') {
    58	      const rule = document.createElement('hr');
    59	      rule.className = 'ledger-card-hr';
    60	      body.appendChild(rule);
    61	      continue;
    62	    }
    63	    const paragraph = document.createElement('p');
    64	    appendInlineNodes(paragraph, block.children, options);
    65	    body.appendChild(paragraph);
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  return body;
    69	}
````

The central conclusion is now firm: the previous fix protected gesture target IDs, but it did not establish ownership or ordering for asynchronous ledger writes. A stale refresh response can still replace a newer `activeLedger`; the pointer snapshot then continues against a different ledger object. I’m recording a single reconciliation architecture in the audit card rather than proposing another layer of state gates.

````text
## A. Repository Intent

1. **State ownership.** The filesystem is the durable source of truth for ledger records, card Markdown, and thread Markdown. The frontend owns transient interaction state including `state.selection`, `state.pointer`, `state.viewport`, thread controls, focus, and in-progress geometry.
2. **Refresh contract.** Specs `b7e4dfd1`, `689842e0`, and `9d1b7c36` require external content refresh to preserve the active route, viewport, selected thread, and live canvas interaction state.
3. **Interaction contract.** A refresh completing during `Ctrl+D`, drag, resize, pan, wheel zoom, card selection, and thread control input must not replace newer local state with an older server snapshot.

---

## B. Current Iteration Intent

1. **Zone reviewed.** `ledger-cli card-context` resolved this card to zone `zone-4391b4b5-1f2e-47dc-aa1e-e14d606e5f28`, labelled `Rollbacks`. `ledger-cli zone-cards` returned seven cards, and every returned card was read in full.
2. **Claimed repair.** The zone records a pointer selection snapshot, explicit movement and commit selection, remounted resize-target resolution, same-ledger selection pruning, thread refresh selection protection, race tests, and commit `1d09580` (`Preserve canvas gesture identity across refresh`).
3. **Observed regression.** Codex activity still coincides with `Ctrl+D` geometry rollback, drag and resize interruption, visible selection swaps, a canvas selection that needs `Escape` before it becomes clickable again, and model selection in the thread panel being interrupted.

---

## C. Audit Outcome

1. **Verdict.** The implementation is **incomplete**. It freezes gesture target ids, but it does not serialize ledger responses, merge against current local state, isolate thread updates from canvas state, preserve interactive control nodes, drain queued refreshes, and reconcile selection state with selection DOM.
2. **Timer finding.** The `1000` ms timer in `poll-card-skill-run.ts` does not directly reload the canvas. It calls the run-status endpoint. When that read persists newly discovered Codex events, the thread file watcher emits `thread-content-change`; the frontend then reloads the entire active ledger and rebuilds the thread panel.
3. **Fundamental cause.** Multiple async paths can assign `state.activeLedger` without a shared request sequence, route epoch, per-record local geometry revision, and stale-response rejection. Snapshot gates around `state.selection` cannot make those assignments safe.
4. **Required direction.** Replace independent refresh gates with one generation-aware ledger reconciliation coordinator, and make thread-file refresh update only the thread slice and thread-note DOM.

---

## D. Verified Codex Refresh Chain

1. **One-second poll.** `frontend/src/runtime/codex/effect/poll-card-skill-run.ts` schedules `poll()` with a default `1000` ms delay and calls `requestCardSkillRunStatus()`.
2. **Read endpoint writes state.** `backend/src/business/codex/controller/read-card-skill-run-controller.ts` calls `persistRunEvents()`. When new events exist, that function writes the card thread Markdown through `writeThreadNotesFile()` and rewrites the ledger JSON.
3. **Watcher emission.** `backend/src/business/refresh/helper/watch-card-content-files.ts` watches `.decision-os/threads/**/*.md`, debounces for `50` ms, and publishes `card-content-change` with `kind: thread-content` through `/api/ledger-content-events`.
4. **Whole-ledger thread refresh.** `requestThreadContentRefresh()` enters `reloadThreadContent()`, which calls `loadActiveLedgerState()` before rendering the thread panel. A thread-note update therefore enters the same ledger replacement path used by canvas refresh.
5. **Control remount.** `renderThreadPanel()` calls `renderThreadActions()`. That function runs `actions.replaceChildren()` and creates new model and effort `<select>` nodes every time. A refresh between pointer down and the `change` event detaches the active control, closes its popup, loses focus, and prevents the intended value from reaching `threadCodexModel`.
6. **Card widget remount.** Ledger refresh calls `renderCanvasSurface()`. `patchLedgerCard()` and `renderLedgerCardDetailLayer()` replace card children and create a new Codex run widget, including new model and effort controls. The poller rebinds to the new widget, but active DOM focus and an open select interaction cannot transfer to the replacement node.

---

## E. Critical Findings

1. **Critical - stale GET can undo a newer geometry PATCH.** `loadActiveLedgerState()` captures `localLedger = state.activeLedger` before `await fetch()`, then unconditionally assigns a merge based on that captured object after the response resolves. `commitActiveLedgerMutation()` follows the same capture-before-await pattern. Neither path records request order and neither rejects an older response after a newer response has been applied.
2. **Critical - `Ctrl+D` exposes the stale snapshot.** `resizeSelectedCardsToContent()` updates DOM geometry and returns a payload, but it does not patch the matching records in `state.activeLedger`. `resizeSelectedCardsController()` then awaits `patch-geometry`. A ledger GET started before that PATCH can retain the old `localLedger`, resolve after the successful PATCH, and restore the pre-`Ctrl+D` geometry.
3. **Critical - gesture identity is stable while the ledger object is not.** `state.pointer.selectionSnapshot` freezes ids used by `moveSelected()` and `commitSelectedLedgerGeometry()`. It does not freeze the geometry base and does not prevent `loadActiveLedgerState()` from replacing `state.activeLedger` between pointer moves. Later deltas can therefore apply to a different ledger object than earlier deltas.
4. **High - pointer operand and visible selection remain conflated.** On a same-ledger load, `loadActiveLedgerState()` assigns `state.selection` from `state.pointer.selectionSnapshot`. The snapshot should control movement and commit only. Copying it into visible selection can overwrite a newer visible selection and reproduces the selection-swap behavior the original audit intended to remove.
5. **High - thread refresh can desynchronize selection state and DOM.** `reloadThreadContent()` allows `loadActiveLedgerState()` to change `state.selection`, then calls `renderThreadPanel()` without `renderSelectionState()`. A pruned, cleared, and pointer-restored selection can disagree with `.selected` classes on canvas nodes. When state still includes the clicked card, `shouldPreservePointerSelection()` makes pointer down skip `selectTarget()` and click-like pointer up returns without repainting selection. `Escape` clears state and calls `renderCanvasSurface()`, which explains the reported recovery sequence.
6. **High - refresh failures destroy live state.** The missing-endpoint and failed-response branches in `loadActiveLedgerState()` clear `state.activeLedger`, `state.activeLedgerId`, and `state.selection` without finishing `state.pointer`. A transient failed status-related refresh can leave a live pointer session attached to cleared ledger state.
7. **High - route and response order are unchecked.** `loadActiveLedgerState()` captures the endpoint and `ledgerStateId`, awaits the network, then applies the response without verifying the current route and active ledger identity. A response from a previous route can replace the current route ledger.
8. **High - thread and ledger loads can overlap.** `refreshInFlight` and `threadRefreshInFlight` are separate booleans. A Codex lifecycle `ledger-content-change`, a thread-file event, and `processThreadCodexController()` calling `refreshRuntimeState()` can start concurrent loads that all write `state.activeLedger`.
9. **High - SSE refreshes are unscoped.** `subscribeLedgerContentEvents()` reloads the active surface for received card, thread, and ledger events without validating payload `ledgerId`, the active `threadId`, and ownership of `contentFile`. Progress from a background run in another ledger can therefore interrupt the current ledger.
10. **High - queued refreshes are dropped.** When a reload is in flight, the code sets `state.pendingLedgerContentRefresh` and `state.pendingThreadContentRefresh`. The `finally` blocks only clear the in-flight booleans; they do not execute the pending request. `flushPendingLedgerContentRefresh()` drains these flags only after voice recording, so events received during an ordinary in-flight reload can remain unapplied.
11. **Medium - status polling has an unexpected write side effect.** The run-status GET persists thread events. This client-originated read creates a filesystem watcher echo back into the client refresh system, conflicting with the spec boundary that client-originated updates must not trigger a client refresh.
12. **Medium - merge policy is inconsistent.** Geometry is merged from the `localLedger` captured before the request, while `mergeLocalThreadNotes()` reads the current global `state.activeLedger` at response time. One response therefore combines different local generations without declaring which generation is authoritative.

---

## F. Coverage And Plan Drift

1. **Current checks pass.** The focused runtime command passed `37/37`, the refresh browser spec passed `1/1`, and `npm run typecheck:frontend` exited `0` on `2026-07-10`.
2. **Race ordering is absent.** Existing drag tests resolve the ledger load before the next pointer move and before release. They do not start a GET, apply a newer geometry PATCH, then resolve the older GET last.
3. **`Ctrl+D` is absent.** No test races `resizeSelectedCardsController()` with a thread-driven ledger load and asserts the final `state.activeLedger`, DOM, persisted ledger, and selection.
4. **Codex chain is absent.** No test covers `schedulePoll()` -> `readCardSkillRunController()` -> `persistRunEvents()` -> thread watcher -> SSE -> `reloadThreadContent()` while a model select, canvas selection, drag, and resize interaction is active.
5. **Focus identity is absent.** The thread refresh test uses a fake `addEventListener()` that records no interaction and never asserts that the same model `<select>` node, focus, open interaction, and chosen value survive note updates.
6. **Failure and queue paths are absent.** No test covers refresh failure during a pointer session, reverse-order concurrent loads, route change during load, and pending refresh drainage.
7. **Browser proof is narrower than reported.** The browser test waits for manual refresh completion before continuing the drag and releasing. It asserts the committed target id, but it does not reproduce a stale response arriving after the geometry commit. It starts the server from the repo workspace instead of the isolated temporary workspace required by the completeness card.

---

## G. Selected Remediation Architecture

1. **Create one reconciliation coordinator.** Add `frontend/src/runtime/ledger/effect/reconcile-active-ledger-state.ts` as the only path allowed to replace or merge `state.activeLedger`. Route `loadActiveLedgerState()`, `refreshRuntimeState()`, `reloadLedgerContent()`, and `commitActiveLedgerMutation()` through it.
2. **Track request generation.** Add a backend-supplied monotonic ledger revision, then store a transient route epoch, monotonically increasing client request sequence, last applied server revision, and per-record geometry revision in runtime state. Reject a route-epoch mismatch. Reject a server revision older than the applied base. Within the same server revision, reject an obsolete client sequence.
3. **Track local geometry revisions.** Every drag, resize, `Ctrl+D`, paste, and geometry-producing effect must patch `state.activeLedger` immediately and increment the affected card, zone, and group revision before network work begins.
4. **Merge against response-time state.** A ledger response must merge into the current `state.activeLedger`, not the object captured at request start. For each record, retain current geometry when its local revision advanced after the request snapshot; accept server geometry when it did not advance.
5. **Acknowledge mutations by submitted revision.** A `patch-geometry` response may confirm the submitted revision for its target ids. Geometry changed again after submission remains local and is not overwritten by the response.
6. **Separate pointer operand from visible selection.** Keep `state.pointer.selectionSnapshot` private to gesture movement and commit. Same-ledger refresh must prune the current visible `state.selection` without assigning the pointer snapshot into it.
7. **Refresh only the thread slice.** Validate the event ledger and thread ownership, then replace `reloadThreadContent()` with a thread-slice loader that merges `notes` and `deletedNoteIds` for the active thread into the current ledger. It must not call `loadActiveLedgerState()` and must call `renderThreadNotes()` without rebuilding canvas state.
8. **Preserve thread controls.** Mount thread model and effort controls once per thread id, store their transient values in `state.threadCodexPreferencesByThreadId`, and update the note list independently. `renderThreadActions()` must not call `replaceChildren()` for an unchanged thread.
9. **Make status reads read-only.** Move Codex event persistence into backend run lifecycle ingestion and publish one targeted thread-content event after coalesced persisted events. `readCardSkillRunController()` must not write thread Markdown and ledger JSON.
10. **Drain refresh queues.** On reload completion, consume the pending ledger reasons, pending changed content files, and pending thread flag before releasing the coordinator. Repeated events may coalesce, but the latest state must receive one final reconciliation.
11. **Fail without clearing interaction state.** A transient fetch failure must retain the current ledger, selection, pointer, viewport, and focused controls while recording failure telemetry. Definitive route transitions clear state through the navigation controller before the new route load.

---

## H. Acceptance Criteria

1. **`Ctrl+D` ordering.** Start a thread-driven GET with old geometry, run `Ctrl+D`, apply the successful PATCH response, then resolve the old GET. Final frontend geometry, rendered geometry, and ledger geometry must equal the `Ctrl+D` result.
2. **Drag ordering.** Start drag on `card-a`, start a GET, move again, release, apply the PATCH response, then resolve the old GET. Only `card-a` moves and the final position equals the full pointer delta.
3. **Resize ordering.** Start resize on a card, remount its inner DOM, complete a newer resize PATCH, then resolve an older GET. The same card keeps the final width and height.
4. **Selection consistency.** After every refresh completion, `state.selection` and canvas `.selected` classes must represent the same ids. A selected card must remain directly clickable without an `Escape` recovery step.
5. **Thread model continuity.** While Codex events arrive, the active thread model `<select>` must retain node identity, focus, current value, and the next committed selection.
6. **Status read contract.** Repeated run-status GET requests must not change the thread Markdown mtime, ledger JSON mtime, and SSE event count.
7. **Failure continuity.** A failed refresh during drag, resize, pan, and thread model input must leave the active interaction usable and allow normal completion.
8. **Queue drainage.** Multiple ledger and thread events received during one in-flight load must produce one final refresh containing the latest server state and every changed card content file that still requires content sizing.
9. **Route safety.** A response started on `/bugs` must not mutate runtime state after navigation to another ledger route.
10. **SSE scope.** A Codex progress event for an inactive ledger and inactive thread must leave the load count, `state.activeLedger`, canvas DOM, and thread-control DOM unchanged.
11. **Browser proof.** Run the Codex thread-event, `Ctrl+D`, drag, resize, selection, and model-control races against an isolated temporary `.decision-os` workspace.

---

---

## J. Operator Decision Summary

1. **Decision.** Treat `1d09580` as a partial mitigation, not a complete rollback fix.
2. **Next implementation batch.** Implement the single reconciliation architecture in Section G before adding more selection gates.
3. **Status boundary.** This run performed the requested audit and durable card rewrite. It did not change product code and did not change the card `todo` status.
````

`````text
<!-- decision-os:note {"id":"codex-codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa-line-204","timestamp":"2026-07-10T06:30:14.199Z","status":"Codex message","codexRunId":"codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa","codexLine":"204","codexKind":"agent_message","codexEventType":"item.completed","codexItemId":"item_104"} -->

The central conclusion is now firm: the previous fix protected gesture target IDs, but it did not establish ownership or ordering for asynchronous ledger writes. A stale refresh response can still replace a newer `activeLedger`; the pointer snapshot then continues against a different ledger object. I’m recording a single reconciliation architecture in the audit card rather than proposing another layer of state gates.
`````

## A. Audit Result

1. **Verdict.** The rollback implementation is incomplete. Commit `1d09580` preserves gesture target ids, but async ledger GET and PATCH responses remain unordered and an older response can replace newer geometry, selection, and ledger state.
2. **One-second timer.** The `1000` ms Codex poll does not directly refresh the canvas. When its status read discovers new events, the backend persists them to thread Markdown, the watcher emits SSE, and `reloadThreadContent()` reloads the entire active ledger. That indirect chain is the recurring trigger.

---

## B. Verified Omissions

1. **Geometry rollback.** `Ctrl+D` updates DOM geometry without first updating `state.activeLedger`; a stale GET that resolves after the geometry PATCH can restore the old size.
2. **Broken selection.** Thread refresh can change `state.selection` without repainting canvas selection classes. The next click can skip `selectTarget()` because state still treats the card as selected; `Escape` clears state and repaints, matching the reported workaround.
3. **Model control interruption.** Every thread refresh runs `actions.replaceChildren()` and recreates the model and effort selects. A refresh detaches the active select before its change commits.
4. **Additional gaps.** Refresh failures clear live state, route identity is not rechecked after awaits, ledger and thread loads overlap, SSE events are not scoped to the active ledger and thread, and pending refreshes are not drained after an ordinary in-flight load.

---

## C. Durable Result

1. **Audit card updated.** `.decision-os/cards/bugs/card-4132d930-9286-432e-9c6d-5d542fc1c69b.md` now contains the complete evidence register, one selected revision-aware reconciliation architecture, acceptance criteria, and source register for all seven cards in the `Rollbacks` zone.
2. **Verification.** Focused runtime tests passed `37/37`, the current browser refresh proof passed `1/1`, and `npm run typecheck:frontend` exited `0`. The card documents the race and focus paths those checks do not cover.
3. **Scope.** No product code, ledger JSON, unrelated note, and card status was changed.

``````text
# Thread Codex Run
``````

```````text
Codex run: `codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa`

## A. Audit Result

1. **Verdict.** Commit `1d09580` is a partial mitigation. Gesture target ids are stable, but async ledger GET and PATCH responses remain unordered and can restore older geometry and selection state.
2. **Codex trigger.** The `1000` ms widget poll indirectly triggers refresh when the run-status read persists new thread events, the file watcher emits SSE, and the frontend reloads the whole active ledger.
3. **Selected correction.** Use one revision-aware reconciliation coordinator, a thread-only refresh slice, stable thread controls, read-only status requests, and a trailing refresh queue.

---

## B. Durable Output

1. **Audit card.** `.decision-os/cards/bugs/card-4132d930-9286-432e-9c6d-5d542fc1c69b.md` contains the full evidence register, remediation architecture, acceptance criteria, and source register.
2. **Scope.** All seven cards in the `Rollbacks` zone were read through `ledger-cli` discovery. No unrelated note was treated and no card status was changed.

---

## C. Verification

1. **Runtime checks.** Focused refresh, gesture, thread, canvas, and Codex tests passed `37/37`.
2. **Browser check.** The current refresh interaction spec passed `1/1`.
3. **Typecheck.** `npm run typecheck:frontend` exited `0`.
4. **Coverage finding.** Current checks do not exercise stale GET after newer PATCH, `Ctrl+D` plus Codex thread refresh, active model-control remount, failed refresh continuity, unscoped SSE, and pending queue drainage.
```````

Audit complete. The implementation is incomplete.

Key findings:

- The 1-second Codex poll indirectly triggers whole-ledger reloads when new run events are persisted.
- Unordered GET and PATCH responses can restore stale geometry after `Ctrl+D`, drag, and resize.
- Selection state can diverge from canvas selection classes, explaining the required `Escape` recovery.
- Thread refresh recreates model and effort controls, interrupting active selection.
- SSE events are unscoped, queued refreshes can be dropped, and failed refreshes clear live state.

The full evidence register and selected reconciliation architecture are in the [audit card](/home/jbb/dev/EditorBP/decision-os/.decision-os/cards/bugs/card-4132d930-9286-432e-9c6d-5d542fc1c69b.md:1). The [thread reply](/home/jbb/dev/EditorBP/decision-os/.decision-os/threads/bugs/thread-card-4132d930-9286-432e-9c6d-5d542fc1c69b.md:10754) and [run summary](/home/jbb/dev/EditorBP/decision-os/.decision-os/runs/codex-skills/bugs/codex-skill-1783664442785-5ab9e3aa.md:1) are updated.

Verification passed: runtime `37/37`, browser `1/1`, and frontend typecheck. No product code, ledger JSON, or card status was changed.
